# OmniForge Template OS — source bundle

Every file of this app, in order: the file name, then its code.

```jsonc
{
  "name": "base44-app",
  "site": {
    "installCommand": "npm install",
    "buildCommand": "npm run build",
    "serveCommand": "npm run dev",
    "outputDirectory": "./dist"
  }
}
```

## 8. base44/functions/approveDirection/entry.ts

```ts
import { createClientFromRequest } from 'npm:@base44/sdk@0.8.54';

const CODE = /^[A-HJ-NP-Z2-9]{10}$/;

export default async function (req) {
  try {
    const base44 = createClientFromRequest(req);
    const user = await base44.auth.me().catch((e) => (e.status === 401 ? null : Promise.reject(e)));
    if (!user) return Response.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });

    const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
    const code = String(body.shareCode || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    const name = String(body.name || '').trim().slice(0, 120);
    const note = String(body.note || '').trim().slice(0, 500);
    if (!CODE.test(code)) return Response.json({ error: 'A valid share code is required.' }, { status: 400 });
    if (name.length < 2) return Response.json({ error: 'A full name is required to sign.' }, { status: 400 });

    const { items } = await base44.asServiceRole.entities.Project.filter({ share_code: code }, { limit: 1 });
    const project = (items || [])[0];
    if (!project) return Response.json({ error: 'That share link does not match a project.' }, { status: 404 });

    const updated = await base44.asServiceRole.entities.Project.update(project.id, {
      status: 'approved',
      approval_name: name,
      approval_note: note,
      approved_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    });

    return Response.json({
      status: updated.status,
      approval_name: updated.approval_name,
      approval_note: updated.approval_note,
      approved_at: updated.approved_at,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return Response.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
```

## 9. base44/functions/generateBrief/entry.ts

```ts
import { createClientFromRequest } from 'npm:@base44/sdk@0.8.54';

const FONTS = ["editorial", "modern", "grotesk", "contrast", "geometric", "soft", "technical"];

const SCHEMA = {
  type: "object",
  properties: {
    title: { type: "string" },
    positioning: { type: "string" },
    audience: { type: "string" },
    copy: {
      type: "object",
      properties: {
        headline: { type: "string" },
        subhead: { type: "string" },
        cta: { type: "string" },
      },
      required: ["headline", "subhead", "cta"],
    },
    buyer_paths: {
      type: "array",
      items: {
        type: "object",
        properties: {
          name: { type: "string" },
          steps: { type: "array", items: { type: "string" } },
        },
        required: ["name", "steps"],
      },
    },
    tokens: {
      type: "object",
      properties: {
        bg: { type: "string" },
        primary: { type: "string" },
        accent: { type: "string" },
        font: { type: "string" },
        radius: { type: "number" },
        space: { type: "number" },
        motion: { type: "number" },
      },
      required: ["bg", "primary", "accent", "font"],
    },
    modules: { type: "array", items: { type: "string" } },
    sections: { type: "array", items: { type: "string" } },
    qa_checklist: { type: "array", items: { type: "string" } },
  },
  required: ["title", "positioning", "audience", "copy", "buyer_paths", "tokens", "modules", "sections", "qa_checklist"],
};

export default async function (req) {
  try {
    const base44 = createClientFromRequest(req);
    const user = await base44.auth.me().catch((e) => (e.status === 401 ? null : Promise.reject(e)));
    if (!user) return Response.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });

    const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
    const business = String(body.prompt || "").trim().slice(0, 600);
    const family = String(body.family || "").slice(0, 40);
    const tier = String(body.tier || "").slice(0, 40);
    if (business.length < 8) {
      return Response.json({ error: "Tell the studio a little more about the business." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const prompt = `You are the art director of OmniForge, a template operating system. From the business description
below, produce ONE structured design brief. Be specific and concrete, never generic marketing filler.

Business description: "${business}"
${family ? `Template family already chosen: ${family}.` : ""}
${tier ? `Tier: ${tier}.` : ""}

Rules:
- title: the business name only, or a short working title if none is given.
- positioning: one sentence, max 28 words, describing what it is and who it is for.
- audience: one sentence on the actual buyer, max 24 words.
- copy: a headline under 12 words, a subhead under 24 words, and a call to action of 2-4 words, all in the voice of this business.
- buyer_paths: 2 or 3 named journeys, each with 2-4 short steps.
- tokens: a real palette for this business as 6-digit hex strings.
  bg must be either a light neutral (like #FFFFFF, #FBF7F0) or a deep dark (like #0B0F14, #14100C).
  primary is the brand colour used for buttons and must read clearly against bg.
  accent is a secondary colour. font must be exactly one of: ${FONTS.join(", ")}.
  radius in whole px (0-28), space between 0.78 and 1.4, motion as an integer 0-3.
- modules: 4-6 module names this business needs, in the order they appear.
- sections: the page order, one line per section, max 7 lines.
- qa_checklist: 4-6 checks a designer would sign off before this goes live.`;

    const brief = await base44.asServiceRole.integrations.Core.InvokeLLM({
      prompt,
      response_json_schema: SCHEMA,
    });

    if (!brief || !brief.title) {
      return Response.json({ error: "The studio could not read that brief. Try rephrasing it." }, { status: 502 });
    }
    return Response.json({ brief });
  } catch (error) {
    return Response.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
```

## 10. base44/functions/generateImages/entry.ts

```ts
import { createClientFromRequest } from 'npm:@base44/sdk@0.8.54';

const STYLE = "editorial photography, natural light, shallow depth of field, no text, no logos, no watermark";

const palette = (tokens) => {
  const t = tokens || {};
  return [t.bg, t.primary, t.accent].filter((c) => typeof c === "string").join(", ") || "neutral";
};

export default async function (req) {
  try {
    const base44 = createClientFromRequest(req);
    const user = await base44.auth.me().catch((e) => (e.status === 401 ? null : Promise.reject(e)));
    if (!user) return Response.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });

    const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
    const kind = body.kind === "brief" ? "brief" : "asset";

    if (kind === "asset") {
      const prompt = String(body.prompt || "").trim().slice(0, 400);
      if (prompt.length < 6) return Response.json({ error: "Describe the image you want." }, { status: 400 });
      const image = await base44.asServiceRole.integrations.Core.GenerateImage({
        prompt: `${prompt}. ${STYLE}.`,
      });
      if (!image || !image.url) return Response.json({ error: "The studio did not return an image." }, { status: 502 });
      return Response.json({ url: image.url, prompt });
    }

    const briefId = String(body.briefId || "").trim();
    if (!briefId) return Response.json({ error: "briefId is required" }, { status: 400 });

    const brief = await base44.entities.Brief.get(briefId).catch((e) => (e.status === 404 ? null : Promise.reject(e)));
    if (!brief) return Response.json({ error: "Not found" }, { status: 404 });

    const subject = brief.title || "the brand";
    const scene = (brief.positioning || "").slice(0, 180);
    const colours = palette(brief.tokens);

    const [hero, product] = await Promise.allSettled([
      base44.asServiceRole.integrations.Core.GenerateImage({
        prompt: `Hero image for ${subject}. ${scene}. Wide cinematic composition with generous negative space on the left, palette of ${colours}. ${STYLE}.`,
      }),
      base44.asServiceRole.integrations.Core.GenerateImage({
        prompt: `Product photograph for ${subject}. A single hero product or dish presented on a plain surface, centred, palette of ${colours}. ${STYLE}.`,
      }),
    ]);

    const images = {};
    if (hero.status === "fulfilled" && hero.value?.url) images.hero = hero.value.url;
    if (product.status === "fulfilled" && product.value?.url) images.product = product.value.url;
    if (!images.hero && !images.product) {
      return Response.json({ error: "The image studio did not return anything. Try again." }, { status: 502 });
    }
    return Response.json({ images });
  } catch (error) {
    return Response.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
```

## 12. src/App.jsx

```jsx
import { Toaster } from "@/components/ui/toaster"
import { QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { queryClientInstance } from '@/lib/query-client'
import { BrowserRouter as Router, Route, Routes, Navigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Toaster as Sonner } from "@/components/ui/sonner";
import PageNotFound from './lib/PageNotFound';
import { AuthProvider, useAuth } from '@/lib/AuthContext';
import UserNotRegisteredError from '@/components/UserNotRegisteredError';
import ScrollToTop from './components/ScrollToTop';
import ProtectedRoute from '@/components/ProtectedRoute';
import Shell from '@/components/os/Shell';
import Showcase from '@/pages/Showcase';
import TemplateLibrary from '@/pages/TemplateLibrary';
import TemplateEditor from '@/pages/TemplateEditor';
import Modules from '@/pages/Modules';
import Configurator from '@/pages/Configurator';
import Projects from '@/pages/Projects';
import Compare from '@/pages/Compare';
import BriefStudio from '@/pages/BriefStudio';
import AssetStudio from '@/pages/AssetStudio';
import ClientShare from '@/pages/ClientShare';
import SourceBundle from '@/pages/SourceBundle';
import Login from '@/pages/Login';
import Register from '@/pages/Register';
import ForgotPassword from '@/pages/ForgotPassword';
import ResetPassword from '@/pages/ResetPassword';

const AuthenticatedApp = () => {
  const { isLoadingAuth, isLoadingPublicSettings, authError, navigateToLogin } = useAuth();

  // Show loading spinner while checking app public settings or auth
  if (isLoadingPublicSettings || isLoadingAuth) {
    return (
      <div className="fixed inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
        <div className="w-8 h-8 border-4 border-slate-200 border-t-slate-800 rounded-full animate-spin"></div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // Handle authentication errors
  if (authError) {
    if (authError.type === 'user_not_registered') {
      return <UserNotRegisteredError />;
    } else if (authError.type === 'auth_required') {
      // Redirect to login automatically
      navigateToLogin();
      return null;
    }
  }

  // Render the main app
  return (
    <Routes>
      <Route path="/login" element={<Login />} />
      <Route path="/register" element={<Register />} />
      <Route path="/forgot-password" element={<ForgotPassword />} />
      <Route path="/reset-password" element={<ResetPassword />} />
      <Route element={<ProtectedRoute unauthenticatedElement={<Navigate to="/login" replace />} />}>
        <Route element={<Shell />}>
          <Route path="/" element={<Showcase />} />
          <Route path="/templates" element={<TemplateLibrary />} />
          <Route path="/templates/:slug" element={<TemplateEditor />} />
          <Route path="/modules" element={<Modules />} />
          <Route path="/configurator" element={<Configurator />} />
          <Route path="/projects" element={<Projects />} />
          <Route path="/compare" element={<Compare />} />
          <Route path="/brief" element={<BriefStudio />} />
          <Route path="/assets" element={<AssetStudio />} />
          <Route path="/source" element={<SourceBundle />} />
        </Route>
        <Route path="/p/:code" element={<ClientShare />} />
      </Route>
      <Route path="*" element={<PageNotFound />} />
    </Routes>
  );
};


function App() {

  return (
    <AuthProvider>
      <QueryClientProvider client={queryClientInstance}>
        <Router>
          <ScrollToTop />
          <AuthenticatedApp />
        </Router>
        <Toaster />
        <Sonner />
      </QueryClientProvider>
    </AuthProvider>
  )
}

export default App
```

```jsx
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight text-foreground">{title}</h1>

        </div>
        <div className="bg-card rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-border p-8">
          {children}
        </div>
        {footer && (
          <p className="text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground mt-6">{footer}</p>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## 16. src/components/ProtectedRoute.jsx

```jsx
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { Outlet } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '@/lib/AuthContext';
import UserNotRegisteredError from '@/components/UserNotRegisteredError';

const DefaultFallback = () => (
  <div className="fixed inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
    <div className="w-8 h-8 border-4 border-slate-200 border-t-slate-800 rounded-full animate-spin"></div>
  </div>
);

export default function ProtectedRoute({ fallback = <DefaultFallback />, unauthenticatedElement }) {
  const { isAuthenticated, isLoadingAuth, authChecked, authError, checkUserAuth } = useAuth();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!authChecked && !isLoadingAuth) {
      checkUserAuth();
    }
  }, [authChecked, isLoadingAuth, checkUserAuth]);

  if (isLoadingAuth || !authChecked) {
    return fallback;
  }

  if (authError) {
    if (authError.type === 'user_not_registered') {
      return <UserNotRegisteredError />;
    }
    return unauthenticatedElement;
  }

  if (!isAuthenticated) {
    return unauthenticatedElement;
  }

  return <Outlet />;
}
```

## 17. src/components/ScrollToTop.jsx

```jsx
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { useLocation, useNavigationType } from "react-router-dom";

const getHashId = (hash) => {
  const rawId = hash.slice(1);

  try {
    return decodeURIComponent(rawId);
  } catch {
    return rawId;
  }
};

export default function ScrollToTop() {
  const { pathname, hash } = useLocation();
  const navigationType = useNavigationType();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (navigationType === "POP") return;

    if (hash) {
      const id = getHashId(hash);
      const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
        document.getElementById(id)?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth" });
      }, 50);
      return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
    }

    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, left: 0, behavior: "instant" });
  }, [pathname, hash, navigationType]);

  return null;
}
```

## 20. src/components/customizer/ImagesTab.jsx

```jsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useTokens } from "@/context/TokensContext";
import { TEMPLATE_SLOTS } from "@/lib/tokens";
import { IMG } from "@/lib/images";
import { listAssets } from "@/lib/store";

export default function ImagesTab() {
  const { slug, tokens, setImage } = useTokens();
  const slots = TEMPLATE_SLOTS[slug] || [];
  const [active, setActive] = useState(slots[0]?.[0]);
  const { data: assets = [] } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["assets"], queryFn: listAssets });

  if (!slots.length) {
    return <p className="text-sm text-[#8D897F]" data-testid="images-none">This family's grammar is photo-free — it leans on data and type instead.</p>;
  }
  const current = tokens.images?.[active];

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <section>
        <div className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F] mb-3">Image slots</div>
        <div className="space-y-1">
          {slots.map(([k, l]) => {
            const src = tokens.images?.[k] || IMG[k];
            return (
              <button
                key={k}
                data-testid={`image-slot-${k}`}
                onClick={() => setActive(k)}
                className={`w-full flex items-center gap-3 p-1.5 border text-left transition-colors duration-200 ${
                  active === k ? "border-[#D4AF37] bg-[#D4AF37]/[0.06]" : "border-white/[0.06] hover:border-white/25"
                }`}
              >
                <span className="h-9 w-12 bg-white/5 overflow-hidden shrink-0">
                  {src && <img src={src} alt="" className="h-full w-full object-cover" />}
                </span>
                <span className="flex-1 text-sm text-[#CFCBC1]">{l}</span>
                {tokens.images?.[k] && <span className="eyebrow text-[#D4AF37] pr-1">AI</span>}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </section>

      <section>
        <div className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F] mb-3">Replace with</div>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
          <button
            data-testid="image-use-original"
            onClick={() => setImage(active, null)}
            className={`aspect-square border text-[10px] eyebrow ${!current ? "border-[#D4AF37] text-[#D4AF37]" : "border-white/10 text-[#8D897F] hover:border-white/30"}`}
          >
            Original
          </button>
          {assets.map((a) => (
            <button
              key={a.id}
              data-testid={`image-use-asset-${a.id}`}
              onClick={() => setImage(active, a.url)}
              title={a.prompt}
              className={`aspect-square border overflow-hidden ${current === a.url ? "border-[#D4AF37] ring-1 ring-[#D4AF37]" : "border-white/10 hover:border-white/30"}`}
            >
              <img src={a.url} alt={a.prompt} className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <Link to="/assets" className="block text-xs text-[#8D897F] hover:text-[#D4AF37] mt-4" data-testid="images-generate-link">
          {assets.length ? "Generate more in the AI asset studio →" : "No AI images yet — generate one in the AI asset studio →"}
        </Link>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```jsx
import { useTokens } from "@/context/TokensContext";
import { ACCENT_SWATCHES, TEMPLATE_TOKENS } from "@/lib/tokens";
import { Chip } from "./controls";

const RADII = [0, 4, 12, 24];
const DENSITY = [["Airy", 1.45], ["Standard", 1], ["Dense", 0.7]];

export default function PresetsTab() {

  return (

          {presets.map(([name, p], i) => {
            const t = { ...defaults, ...p };
            return (
              <button key={name} data-testid={`preset-${i}`} onClick={() => apply(t)} className="border border-white/10 hover:border-[#D4AF37] transition-colors duration-200 text-left">
                <div className="h-14 flex items-end p-2 gap-1" style={{ background: t.bg }}>
                  <span className="h-4 w-4" style={{ background: t.primary }} />
                  <span className="h-4 w-4" style={{ background: t.accent }} />
                  <span className="h-4 w-4" style={{ background: t.ink }} />
                </div>
                <div className="text-[11px] px-2 py-1.5 text-[#CFCBC1]">{name}</div>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </section>

      <section>
        <div className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F] mb-3">Accent</div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {ACCENT_SWATCHES.map((c) => (
            <button
              key={c}
              data-testid={`swatch-${c.slice(1)}`}
              onClick={() => setToken("primary", c)}
              aria-label={c}
              className={`h-8 w-8 border transition-transform duration-200 hover:-translate-y-0.5 ${
                String(tokens.primary).toLowerCase() === c.toLowerCase() ? "border-[#D4AF37] ring-1 ring-[#D4AF37] ring-offset-2 ring-offset-[#0B0B0A]" : "border-white/20"
              }`}
              style={{ background: c }}
            />
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>

      <section>
        <div className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F] mb-3">Corner radius</div>
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          {RADII.map((r) => (
            <Chip key={r} testid={`radius-chip-${r}`} active={tokens.radius === r} onClick={() => setToken("radius", r)}>{r}px</Chip>
          ))}

      <section>
        <div className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F] mb-3">Density</div>

          ))}
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```jsx
import { useTokens } from "@/context/TokensContext";
import { prestigeScore } from "@/lib/tokens";

export default function PrestigeMeter() {
  const { tokens } = useTokens();
  const { score, grade, checks } = prestigeScore(tokens);
  return (
    <div className="px-5 py-4 border-b border-white/[0.08]" data-testid="prestige-meter">
      <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between">
        <span className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F]">Prestige score</span>
        <span className="font-display text-3xl" data-testid="prestige-score">
          {score}<span className="text-sm text-[#8D897F]"> · {grade}</span>
        </span>
      </div>
      <div className="h-[2px] bg-white/10 mt-2">
        <div className="h-full bg-[#D4AF37] transition-[width] duration-500" style={{ width: `${score}%` }} />
      </div>
      <ul className="mt-3 space-y-1">
        {checks.map((c) => (
          <li key={c.label} className="flex justify-between text-[11px]" data-testid="prestige-check">
            <span className={c.pass ? "text-[#CFCBC1]" : "text-[#FF8A7A]"}>{c.pass ? "✓" : "!"} {c.label}</span>
            <span className="font-mono-os text-[#8D897F]">{c.ratio.toFixed(1)}:1 / {c.target}</span>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## 23. src/components/customizer/ProjectSave.jsx

```jsx
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { useTokens } from "@/context/TokensContext";
import { templateBySlug } from "@/lib/catalog";
import { createProject, listProjects, updateProject } from "@/lib/store";

export default function ProjectSave() {
  const { slug, tokens } = useTokens();
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const [params] = useSearchParams();
  const [target, setTarget] = useState(params.get("project") || "new");
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const { data: projects = [] } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["projects"], queryFn: listProjects });
  const meta = templateBySlug(slug);

  useEffect(() => {
    const p = params.get("project");
    if (p) setTarget(p);
  }, [params]);

  const save = async () => {
    if (!meta) return;
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      if (target === "new") {
        const created = await createProject({
          name: name.trim() || `${meta.name} draft`,
          family: meta.family,
          tier: meta.tier,
          template_slug: slug,
          modules: [],
          tokens,
        });
        toast.success(`Project “${created.name}” created · share code ${created.share_code}`);
        setName("");
        setTarget(created.id);
      } else {
        const updated = await updateProject(target, { template_slug: slug, tokens });
        toast.success(`Tweaks saved to “${updated.name}”`);
      }
      qc.invalidateQueries();
    } catch (e) {
      toast.error(e.message || "The project could not be saved.");
    }
    setBusy(false);
  };

  return (
    <section className="space-y-3 pb-6 border-b border-white/[0.08]" data-testid="project-save-section">
      <div className="eyebrow text-[#D4AF37]">Save tweaks to a project</div>
      <select value={target} onChange={(e) => setTarget(e.target.value)} data-testid="project-save-target" className="os-select">
        <option value="new">+ New project</option>
        {projects.map((p) => (
          <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>
            {p.name}
            {p.template_slug !== slug ? " (switch template)" : ""}
          </option>
        ))}
      </select>
      {target === "new" && (
        <input
          data-testid="project-save-name"
          value={name}
          onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
          placeholder={meta ? `${meta.name} draft` : "Project name"}
          className="os-input"
        />
      )}
      <button data-testid="project-save-button" disabled={busy || !meta} onClick={save} className="w-full foil-bg py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold disabled:opacity-50">
        {busy ? "Saving…" : target === "new" ? "Create project with these tweaks" : "Update project"}
      </button>
    </section>
  );
}
```

## 24. src/components/customizer/SavedTab.jsx

```jsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { useTokens } from "@/context/TokensContext";
import { createTheme, deleteTheme, listThemes } from "@/lib/store";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import ProjectSave from "./ProjectSave";

export default function SavedTab() {
  const { slug, tokens, apply } = useTokens();
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const { data: themes = [] } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["themes", slug], queryFn: () => listThemes(slug) });
  const refresh = () => qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["themes", slug] });

  const save = async () => {
    try {
      await createTheme({ name: name.trim() || `Theme ${themes.length + 1}`, template_slug: slug, tokens });
      setName("");
      refresh();
      toast.success("Theme saved to the OS");
    } catch (e) {
      toast.error(e.message || "That theme could not be saved.");
    }
  };
  const remove = async (id) => {
    await deleteTheme(id);
    refresh();
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <ProjectSave />

      <div className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F] -mb-3">Reusable themes</div>
      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <Input data-testid="theme-name-input" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} placeholder="Name this theme" className="rounded-none bg-transparent border-white/15" />
        <button data-testid="theme-save-button" onClick={save} className="foil-bg px-4 text-xs font-semibold">Save</button>
      </div>
      <div className="space-y-2" data-testid="saved-themes-list">
        {themes.length === 0 && <p className="text-xs text-[#8D897F]">No saved themes for this template yet.</p>}
        {themes.map((t) => (
          <div key={t.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 border border-white/[0.08] p-2" data-testid={`saved-theme-${t.id}`}>
            <div className="flex">
              {["bg", "primary", "accent"].map((k) => <span key={k} className="h-6 w-4" style={{ background: t.tokens?.[k] }} />)}
            </div>
            <button className="flex-1 text-left text-sm hover:text-[#D4AF37] transition-colors duration-200" onClick={() => apply(t.tokens)} data-testid={`apply-theme-${t.id}`}>
              {t.name}
            </button>
            <button onClick={() => remove(t.id)} className="text-[#8D897F] hover:text-red-400" data-testid={`delete-theme-${t.id}`}>
              <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            </button>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## 25. src/components/customizer/StudioTab.jsx

```jsx
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { RotateCcw, Copy } from "lucide-react";
import { useTokens } from "@/context/TokensContext";
import { COLOR_KEYS, FONT_PAIRS, MOTION } from "@/lib/tokens";
import { Fader, ColorRow } from "./controls";

export default function StudioTab() {
  const { tokens, setToken, reset } = useTokens();
  const copy = () => {
    navigator.clipboard?.writeText(JSON.stringify(tokens, null, 2));
    toast("Tokens copied to clipboard");
  };
  return (
    <div className="space-y-8">
      <section>
        <div className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F] mb-1">Colour tokens</div>
        {COLOR_KEYS.map(([k, l]) => (
          <ColorRow key={k} label={l} value={tokens[k]} onChange={(v) => setToken(k, v)} testid={`color-${k}`} />
        ))}
      </section>

      <section>
        <div className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F] mb-3">Font pairing</div>
        <div className="space-y-1">
          {FONT_PAIRS.map((f) => (
            <button
              key={f.id}
              data-testid={`font-${f.id}`}
              onClick={() => setToken("font", f.id)}
              className={`w-full flex items-baseline justify-between px-3 py-2 border transition-colors duration-200 ${
                tokens.font === f.id ? "border-[#D4AF37] bg-[#D4AF37]/[0.06]" : "border-white/[0.06] hover:border-white/25"
              }`}
            >
              <span className="text-xl" style={{ fontFamily: f.head }}>{f.name}</span>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-[#8D897F]" style={{ fontFamily: f.mono }}>{f.head.split(",")[0].replace(/'/g, "")}</span>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>

      <section>
        <div className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F]">Geometry &amp; rhythm</div>
        <Fader label="Radius" value={tokens.radius} display={`${tokens.radius}px`} min={0} max={32} step={1} onChange={(v) => setToken("radius", v)} testid="fader-radius" />
        <Fader label="Density" value={tokens.space} display={`${Number(tokens.space).toFixed(2)}×`} min={0.5} max={1.8} step={0.05} onChange={(v) => setToken("space", v)} testid="fader-density" />
        <Fader label="Motion" value={tokens.motion} display={(MOTION[tokens.motion] || MOTION[0]).label} min={0} max={3} step={1} onChange={(v) => setToken("motion", v)} testid="fader-motion" />
      </section>

      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <button data-testid="studio-reset" onClick={reset} className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 border border-white/15 py-2.5 text-xs hover:border-white/40 transition-colors duration-200">
          <RotateCcw className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />Reset
        </button>
        <button data-testid="studio-copy" onClick={copy} className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 border border-white/15 py-2.5 text-xs hover:border-white/40 transition-colors duration-200">
          <Copy className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />Copy JSON
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## 26. src/components/customizer/controls.jsx

```jsx
import { Slider } from "@/components/ui/slider";

export const Fader = ({ label, value, display, min, max, step, onChange, testid }) => (
  <div className="py-3">
    <div className="flex justify-between eyebrow text-[#8D897F] mb-3">
      <span>{label}</span>
      <span className="text-[#F2EFE6]" data-testid={`${testid}-value`}>{display ?? value}</span>
    </div>
    <Slider className="fader" value={[value]} min={min} max={max} step={step} onValueChange={([v]) => onChange(v)} data-testid={testid} />
  </div>
);

export const Chip = ({ active, children, onClick, testid }) => (
  <button
    data-testid={testid}
    onClick={onClick}
    className={`px-3 py-2 text-xs border transition-colors duration-200 ${
      active ? "border-[#D4AF37] text-[#D4AF37] bg-[#D4AF37]/[0.07]" : "border-white/10 text-[#8D897F] hover:text-[#F2EFE6] hover:border-white/30"
    }`}
  >
    {children}
  </button>
);

export const ColorRow = ({ label, value, onChange, testid }) => (
  <label className="flex items-center gap-3 py-2 border-b border-white/[0.06]">
    <span className="relative h-7 w-7 border border-white/20 overflow-hidden shrink-0" style={{ background: value }}>
      <input type="color" value={value} onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)} className="absolute inset-0 opacity-0 cursor-pointer" data-testid={testid} />
    </span>
    <span className="flex-1 text-sm text-[#CFCBC1]">{label}</span>
    <span className="font-mono-os text-xs text-[#8D897F] uppercase">{value}</span>
  </label>
);
```

## 27. src/components/os/BriefView.jsx

```jsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";
import { CATALOG } from "@/lib/catalog";
import { Image } from "@/components/ui/image";
import { createAsset, updateBrief } from "@/lib/store";

const Block = ({ label, children }) => (
  <div className="bg-[#070707] border border-white/[0.08] p-6">
    <div className="eyebrow text-[#D4AF37] mb-3">{label}</div>
    {children}
  </div>
);

const swatch = (url) => (String(url || "").includes("media.base44.com") ? <Image src={url} alt="" fittingType="fill" className="h-28 w-40 object-cover border border-white/10" /> : <img src={url} alt="" className="h-28 w-40 object-cover border border-white/10" />);

function BriefImages({ briefId, images, onImages, brief }) {
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const run = async () => {
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const res = await base44.functions.invoke("generateImages", { kind: "brief", briefId });
      const data = res.data || {};
      if (data.error) throw new Error(data.error);
      onImages(data.images || {});
      await updateBrief(briefId, { images: data.images || {} });
      await createAsset({ prompt: `${brief?.title || "Brief"} — hero`, url: data.images?.hero, kind: "brief" }).catch(() => {});
      if (data.images?.product) await createAsset({ prompt: `${brief?.title || "Brief"} — product`, url: data.images.product, kind: "brief" }).catch(() => {});
      toast.success("Hero and product images ready — they travel with the brief");
    } catch (e) {
      toast.error(e.message || "The image studio could not answer.");
    }
    setBusy(false);
  };
  return (
    <div className="border border-white/[0.08] p-6 mb-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-6" data-testid="brief-images">
      <div className="flex-1 min-w-[220px]">
        <div className="eyebrow text-[#D4AF37]">Brief images</div>
        <p className="text-sm text-[#8D897F] mt-2">
          {images?.hero
            ? "Applied automatically as the hero and first product photo when you open a template below."
            : "Generate a matching hero and product image from this brief (about 30 seconds)."}
        </p>
      </div>
      {["hero", "product"].map((k) =>
        images?.[k] && !busy ? (
          <figure key={k}>
            {swatch(images[k])}
            <figcaption className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F] mt-1">{k}</figcaption>
          </figure>
        ) : busy ? (
          <div key={k} className="trace h-28 w-40 bg-[#0B0B0A] grid place-items-center eyebrow text-[#8D897F]">{k}…</div>
        ) : null
      )}
      <button onClick={run} disabled={busy} data-testid="brief-generate-images"
        className="border border-[#D4AF37]/60 text-[#D4AF37] px-4 py-2.5 text-sm hover:bg-[#D4AF37]/10 transition-colors duration-200 disabled:opacity-50">
        {busy ? "Generating…" : images?.hero ? "Regenerate images" : "Generate images"}
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function BriefView({ brief, family, briefId, images, onImages }) {
  if (!brief || !brief.title) return null;
  const matches = CATALOG.filter((t) => t.family === family);
  const tk = brief.tokens || {};
  return (
    <div className="rise" data-testid="brief-result">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-4 mb-6">
        <div>
          <div className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F]">Structured brief</div>
          <h2 className="font-display text-4xl mt-2" data-testid="brief-title">{brief.title}</h2>
          <p className="text-[#CFCBC1] mt-2 max-w-2xl">{brief.positioning}</p>
        </div>
        {briefId && matches.length > 0 && (
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-center">
            <span className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F] mr-1">Apply colours &amp; fonts to</span>
            {matches.map((t, i) => (
              <Link key={t.slug} to={`/templates/${t.slug}?brief=${briefId}`} data-testid={`brief-apply-${t.slug}`}
                className={`px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold ${i === 0 ? "foil-bg" : "border border-white/20 hover:border-[#D4AF37] transition-colors duration-200"}`}>
                {t.name} →
              </Link>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {briefId && <BriefImages briefId={briefId} images={images} onImages={onImages} brief={brief} />}

      <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
        <Block label="Audience"><p className="text-sm">{brief.audience}</p></Block>
        <Block label="Copy">
          <p className="font-display text-2xl">{brief.copy?.headline}</p>
          <p className="text-sm text-[#8D897F] mt-1">{brief.copy?.subhead}</p>
          <span className="inline-block mt-3 text-xs border border-white/20 px-3 py-1">{brief.copy?.cta}</span>
        </Block>
        <Block label="Buyer paths">
          {(brief.buyer_paths || []).map((p) => (
            <div key={p.name} className="mb-3">
              <div className="text-sm font-semibold">{p.name}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-[#8D897F]">{(p.steps || []).join(" → ")}</div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </Block>
        <Block label="Tokens">
          <div className="flex gap-2 mb-3">
            {[["bg", tk.bg], ["primary", tk.primary], ["accent", tk.accent]].map(([k, v]) =>
              v ? <span key={k} className="h-10 w-10 border border-white/15" style={{ background: v }} title={k} /> : null
            )}
          </div>
          <div className="text-xs text-[#8D897F] font-mono-os">r{tk.radius} · density {tk.space} · motion {tk.motion} · {tk.font}</div>
        </Block>
        <Block label="Modules">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            {(brief.modules || []).map((m) => <span key={m} className="text-xs border border-white/15 px-2 py-1 font-mono-os">{m}</span>)}
          </div>
        </Block>
        <Block label="Sections">
          <ol className="text-sm space-y-1 list-decimal list-inside">
            {(brief.sections || []).map((s) => <li key={s}>{s}</li>)}
          </ol>
        </Block>
        <Block label="QA checklist">
          <ul className="text-sm space-y-1">
            {(brief.qa_checklist || []).map((s) => <li key={s}>✓ {s}</li>)}
          </ul>
        </Block>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## 28. src/components/os/ExportPanel.jsx

```jsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { Download, ShieldCheck, ShieldAlert } from "lucide-react";
import { prestigeScore } from "@/lib/tokens";
import { editionLabel } from "@/lib/catalog";
import { downloadSite } from "@/lib/exportSite";
import Seal from "./Seal";

export default function ExportPanel({ project, tokens, captureRef, onExported }) {
  const { score, grade, gate, checks } = prestigeScore(tokens);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const blocked = score < gate;

  const run = async () => {
    if (blocked) {
      toast.error(`Prestige score ${score} is below the ${gate} export gate — fix the flagged contrast first.`);
      return;
    }
    const node = captureRef?.current;
    if (!node) return toast.error("The preview is still loading.");
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const bytes = downloadSite(node, tokens, project);
      toast.success(`Exported — one self-contained file (${Math.round(bytes / 1024)} KB)`);
      onExported?.();
    } catch (e) {
      toast.error(e.message || "The export could not be written.");
    }
    setBusy(false);
  };

  return (
    <section className="bg-[#0B0B0A] border border-white/[0.08] p-6" data-testid="export-panel">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start gap-6">
        <Seal seed={project?.share_code || project?.template_slug || "omniforge"} label={project?.name || "OmniForge"} size={64} />
        <div className="flex-1 min-w-[200px]">
          <div className="eyebrow text-[#D4AF37]">Exported edition</div>
          <div className="font-display text-2xl mt-1">{project?.name || "Untitled direction"}</div>
          <div className="text-xs text-[#8D897F] mt-1">{editionLabel(project?.template_slug)} · prestige {score} {grade}</div>
        </div>
        <button onClick={run} disabled={busy} data-testid="export-button"
          className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold disabled:opacity-50 ${blocked ? "border border-[#FF8A7A]/50 text-[#FF8A7A]" : "foil-bg"}`}>
          {blocked ? <ShieldAlert className="h-4 w-4" /> : <Download className="h-4 w-4" />}
          {busy ? "Writing…" : blocked ? `Blocked at ${score}` : "Export self-contained site"}
        </button>
      </div>
      <ul className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-x-8 mt-5">
        {checks.map((c) => (
          <li key={c.label} className="flex justify-between text-[11px] py-1 border-b border-white/[0.06]">
            <span className={c.pass ? "text-[#CFCBC1]" : "text-[#FF8A7A]"}>{c.pass ? <ShieldCheck className="inline h-3 w-3 mr-1" /> : "! "}{c.label}</span>
            <span className="font-mono-os text-[#8D897F]">{c.ratio.toFixed(1)}:1 / {c.target}</span>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      <p className="text-[11px] text-[#8D897F] mt-4">
        One self-contained HTML file: the token variables, the compiled stylesheet and the rendered page inlined. Photography stays linked to its URL.
      </p>
    </section>
  );
}
```

## 29. src/components/os/ImageManifest.jsx

```jsx
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { X, ExternalLink, Download, Copy } from "lucide-react";
import { useTokens } from "@/context/TokensContext";
import { TEMPLATE_SLOTS } from "@/lib/tokens";
import { IMG } from "@/lib/images";
import { Image } from "@/components/ui/image";

const Slot = ({ url, label }) =>
  String(url || "").includes("media.base44.com") ? (
    <Image src={url} alt={label} fittingType="fill" className="w-full aspect-[16/9] object-cover" />
  ) : (
    <img src={url} alt={label} className="w-full aspect-[16/9] object-cover" />
  );

export default function ImageManifest({ onClose }) {
  const { slug, tokens } = useTokens();
  const slots = TEMPLATE_SLOTS[slug] || [];
  const copy = (url) => {
    navigator.clipboard?.writeText(url).then(() => toast("Image URL copied"), () => toast(url)) ?? toast(url);
  };
  const btn = "flex items-center gap-1 border border-white/15 px-2 py-1 text-[11px] hover:border-[#D4AF37] hover:text-[#D4AF37] transition-colors duration-200";
  return (
    <aside className="w-[340px] shrink-0 h-full overflow-y-auto border-l border-white/[0.08] bg-[#0B0B0A] text-[#F2EFE6] rise" data-testid="image-manifest">
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between px-5 pt-5 pb-4 border-b border-white/[0.08]">
        <div>
          <div className="eyebrow text-[#D4AF37]">Image manifest</div>
          <div className="font-display text-2xl mt-1">{slots.length} image slot{slots.length !== 1 && "s"}</div>
        </div>
        <button onClick={onClose} data-testid="image-manifest-close" className="p-1 text-[#8D897F] hover:text-white"><X className="h-4 w-4" /></button>
      </div>
      <div className="p-5 space-y-4">
        {slots.length === 0 && (
          <p className="text-sm text-[#8D897F]" data-testid="image-manifest-empty">This template is photo-free — its grammar is built on data and type.</p>
        )}
        {slots.map(([k, label]) => {
          const ai = tokens.images?.[k];
          const url = ai || IMG[k];
          if (!url) {
            return (
              <div key={k} className="border border-dashed border-white/15 p-4 text-sm text-[#8D897F]" data-testid={`manifest-item-${k}`}>
                {label} — empty slot. Add an AI image in Tune › Images.
              </div>
            );
          }
          return (
            <div key={k} className="border border-white/[0.08]" data-testid={`manifest-item-${k}`}>
              <a href={url} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer"><Slot url={url} label={label} /></a>
              <div className="p-3 space-y-2">
                <div className="flex justify-between gap-2">
                  <span className="text-sm">{label}</span>
                  <span className={`eyebrow ${ai ? "text-[#D4AF37]" : "text-[#8D897F]"}`}>{ai ? "AI asset" : "Stock · Unsplash"}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="font-mono-os text-[10px] text-[#8D897F] break-all line-clamp-2">{url}</div>
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  <a href={url} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" className={btn} data-testid={`manifest-open-${k}`}><ExternalLink className="h-3 w-3" />Open</a>
                  <a href={url} download={`${slug}-${k}`} className={btn} data-testid={`manifest-download-${k}`}><Download className="h-3 w-3" />Download</a>
                  <button onClick={() => copy(url)} className={btn} data-testid={`manifest-copy-${k}`}><Copy className="h-3 w-3" />URL</button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
}
```

## 30. src/components/os/LiveLedger.jsx

```jsx
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { countApproved, countAssets, countProjects, listBriefs, listProjects } from "@/lib/store";
import { editionLabel } from "@/lib/catalog";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";

const Cell = ({ value, label }) => (
  <div className="border-l border-white/[0.12] pl-5">
    <div className="font-display text-3xl leading-none">{value}</div>
    <div className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F] mt-2">{label}</div>
  </div>
);

// The live ledger: what actually exists in this account's OS right now.
export default function LiveLedger({ compact = false }) {
  const { data } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["ledger"],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const [projects, approved, assets, briefs] = await Promise.all([
        countProjects(),
        countApproved(),
        countAssets(),
        listBriefs(),
      ]);
      return { projects, approved, assets, briefs };
    },
  });

  if (!data) return null;

  return (
    <div className={compact ? "px-6 md:px-14 py-10 border-b border-white/[0.08]" : ""}>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-6">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-8 flex-1">
          <Cell value={data.projects} label="Projects saved" />
          <Cell value={data.approved} label="Directions approved" />
          <Cell value={data.briefs.length} label="AI briefs written" />
          <Cell value={data.assets} label="AI images generated" />
        </div>
        <Link to="/projects" className="text-sm text-[#8D897F] hover:text-[#D4AF37] transition-colors duration-200">
          Open projects →
        </Link>
      </div>
      {!compact && data.briefs.length > 0 && (
        <div className="mt-8">
          <div className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F] mb-3">Latest briefs</div>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            {data.briefs.slice(0, 6).map((b) => (
              <Link key={b.id} to={`/brief?brief=${b.id}`} className="text-xs border border-white/15 px-3 py-2 hover:border-[#D4AF37] transition-colors duration-200">
                {b.title} · {b.family}
              </Link>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export const RecentProjects = () => {
  const { data: projects = [] } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["projects"], queryFn: listProjects });
  if (!projects.length) return null;
  return (
    <div className="mt-8">
      <div className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F] mb-3">Recently saved</div>
      <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
        {projects.slice(0, 3).map((p) => (
          <Link key={p.id} to={`/templates/${p.template_slug}?project=${p.id}`} className="border border-white/[0.08] p-4 hover:border-[#D4AF37]/50 transition-colors duration-200">
            <div className="font-display text-2xl">{p.name}</div>
            <div className="text-[11px] text-[#8D897F] mt-1">{editionLabel(p.template_slug)} · {p.status || "draft"}</div>
          </Link>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
```

## 31. src/components/os/ReviewLayer.jsx

```jsx
import { useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { MessageSquarePlus, MessagesSquare, BadgeCheck, X } from "lucide-react";
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";
import { addComment, listComments, setCommentResolved } from "@/lib/store";

const field = "w-full bg-transparent border border-white/15 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-[#F2EFE6] outline-none focus:border-[#D4AF37]";

function DraftPin({ draft, onCancel, onSaved, projectId }) {
  const [author, setAuthor] = useState(localStorage.getItem("of-author") || "");
  const [text, setText] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const submit = async () => {
    if (!text.trim()) return toast.error("Say what should change here.");
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      await addComment({ project_id: projectId, author: author.trim() || "Client", text: text.trim(), x: draft.x, y: draft.y, resolved: false });
      localStorage.setItem("of-author", author.trim());
      onSaved();
    } catch (e) {
      toast.error(e.message || "The note could not be saved.");
    }
    setBusy(false);
  };
  return (
    <div style={{ left: `${draft.x}%`, top: draft.y }} onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()} data-testid="comment-draft"
      className="absolute z-50 w-72 -translate-x-1/2 mt-5 bg-[#070707] border border-[#D4AF37]/60 p-3 space-y-2 text-[#F2EFE6] shadow-2xl">
      <input data-testid="comment-author-input" value={author} onChange={(e) => setAuthor(e.target.value)} placeholder="Your name" className={field} />
      <textarea data-testid="comment-text-input" value={text} onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)} placeholder="What should change here?" rows={3} className={field} />
      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <button data-testid="comment-submit" onClick={submit} disabled={busy} className="flex-1 foil-bg py-2 text-xs font-semibold disabled:opacity-50">
          {busy ? "Pinning…" : "Pin comment"}
        </button>
        <button data-testid="comment-cancel" onClick={onCancel} className="px-3 border border-white/15 text-xs">Cancel</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function CommentList({ comments, onToggle, onClose }) {
  return (
    <aside className="fixed top-16 right-4 z-[60] w-80 max-h-[70vh] overflow-y-auto bg-[#070707]/95 backdrop-blur-xl border border-white/10 text-[#F2EFE6]" data-testid="comment-list">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center p-4 border-b border-white/10">
        <span className="eyebrow text-[#D4AF37]">Review notes · {comments.length}</span>
        <button onClick={onClose} data-testid="comment-list-close"><X className="h-4 w-4" /></button>
      </div>
      {comments.length === 0 && <p className="p-4 text-sm text-[#8D897F]">No comments yet. Use “Comment” and click anywhere on the page.</p>}
      {comments.map((c, i) => (
        <div key={c.id} className={`p-4 border-b border-white/[0.06] ${c.resolved ? "opacity-50" : ""}`} data-testid={`comment-item-${c.id}`}>
          <div className="flex justify-between eyebrow text-[#8D897F]">
            <span>#{i + 1} · {c.author || "Client"}</span>
            <span>{new Date(c.created_date).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-sm mt-2">{c.text}</p>
          <button onClick={() => onToggle(c)} data-testid={`comment-resolve-${c.id}`} className="mt-2 text-[11px] border border-white/15 px-2 py-1 hover:border-[#D4AF37]">
            {c.resolved ? "Reopen" : "Resolve"}
          </button>
        </div>
      ))}
    </aside>
  );
}

function ApproveDialog({ project, onClose, onDone }) {
  const [name, setName] = useState(localStorage.getItem("of-author") || "");
  const [note, setNote] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const submit = async () => {
    if (name.trim().length < 2) return toast.error("A full name is needed to sign.");
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const res = await base44.functions.invoke("approveDirection", { shareCode: project.share_code, name: name.trim(), note: note.trim() });
      const data = res.data || {};
      if (data.error) throw new Error(data.error);
      localStorage.setItem("of-author", name.trim());
      onDone(data);
      toast.success("Direction approved and signed");
    } catch (e) {
      toast.error(e.message || "The approval could not be recorded.");
    }
    setBusy(false);
  };
  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[70] bg-black/60 grid place-items-center p-4" onClick={onClose}>
      <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-[#070707] border border-[#D4AF37]/50 p-6 space-y-3 text-[#F2EFE6]" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()} data-testid="approve-dialog">
        <div className="eyebrow text-[#D4AF37]">Approve this direction</div>
        <p className="font-display text-3xl">{project.name}</p>
        <input data-testid="approve-name-input" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} placeholder="Your full name" className={field} />
        <textarea data-testid="approve-note-input" value={note} onChange={(e) => setNote(e.target.value)} placeholder="Optional note" rows={3} className={field} />
        <button data-testid="approve-submit" onClick={submit} disabled={busy} className="w-full foil-bg py-3 text-sm font-semibold disabled:opacity-50">
          {busy ? "Signing…" : "Sign approval"}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function ReviewLayer({ project }) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const layer = useRef(null);
  const [mode, setMode] = useState(false);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState(null);
  const [list, setList] = useState(false);
  const [approving, setApproving] = useState(false);
  const [approval, setApproval] = useState(project.approval_name ? project : null);
  const key = useMemo(() => ["comments", project.id], [project.id]);

  const { data: comments = [] } = useQuery({ queryKey: key, queryFn: () => listComments(project.id) });
  const refresh = () => qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: key });

  const place = (e) => {
    if (!mode) return;
    const r = layer.current.getBoundingClientRect();
    setDraft({ x: ((e.clientX - r.left) / r.width) * 100, y: e.clientY - r.top });
  };
  const toggle = async (c) => {
    try {
      await setCommentResolved(c.id, !c.resolved);
      refresh();
    } catch (e) {
      toast.error(e.message || "That note could not be updated.");
    }
  };
  const open = comments.filter((c) => !c.resolved).length;
  const tool = "flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 text-xs border transition-colors duration-200";

  return (
    <>
      <div ref={layer} onClick={place} data-testid="review-layer" className={`absolute inset-0 z-40 ${mode ? "cursor-crosshair bg-black/[0.05]" : "pointer-events-none"}`}>
        {comments.map((c, i) => (
          <button
            key={c.id}
            style={{ left: `${c.x}%`, top: c.y }}
            onClick={(e) => {
              e.stopPropagation();
              setList(true);
            }}
            data-testid={`comment-pin-${c.id}`}
            className={`pointer-events-auto absolute -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-7 w-7 rounded-full grid place-items-center text-xs font-bold shadow-lg ${c.resolved ? "bg-white/40 text-black" : "foil-bg"}`}
          >
            {i + 1}
          </button>
        ))}
        {draft && (
          <DraftPin
            draft={draft}
            projectId={project.id}
            onCancel={() => setDraft(null)}
            onSaved={() => {
              setDraft(null);
              setMode(false);
              refresh();
              toast.success("Comment pinned");
            }}
          />
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="fixed top-4 right-4 z-[60] flex flex-wrap gap-2 bg-[#070707]/90 backdrop-blur-xl border border-white/10 p-1 text-[#F2EFE6]" data-testid="review-toolbar">
        <button onClick={() => { setMode(!mode); setDraft(null); }} data-testid="comment-mode-toggle"
          className={`${tool} ${mode ? "bg-[#D4AF37] text-[#070707] border-[#D4AF37]" : "border-white/15"}`}>
          <MessageSquarePlus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />{mode ? "Click to pin" : "Comment"}
        </button>
        <button onClick={() => setList(!list)} data-testid="comment-list-toggle" className={`${tool} border-white/15`}>
          <MessagesSquare className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />{open} open
        </button>
        {approval ? (
          <span className={`${tool} border-[#C6FF3D]/50 text-[#C6FF3D]`} data-testid="approval-status">
            <BadgeCheck className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />Approved · {approval.approval_name}
          </span>
        ) : (
          <button onClick={() => setApproving(true)} data-testid="approve-open" className={`${tool} foil-bg border-transparent font-semibold`}>
            <BadgeCheck className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />Approve
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      {list && <CommentList comments={comments} onToggle={toggle} onClose={() => setList(false)} />}
      {approving && (
        <ApproveDialog
          project={project}
          onClose={() => setApproving(false)}
          onDone={(p) => {
            setApproval({ ...project, ...p });
            setApproving(false);
            qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["projects"] });
          }}
        />
      )}
    </>
  );
}
```

## 32. src/components/os/Seal.jsx

```jsx
const hash = (s) => {
  let h = 2166136261;
  for (const ch of s) {
    h ^= ch.charCodeAt(0);
    h = Math.imul(h, 16777619);
  }
  return h >>> 0;
};

export default function Seal({ seed, label = "", size = 56, color = "#D4AF37", testid = "project-seal" }) {
  const h = hash(seed || "omniforge");
  const petals = 6 + (h % 7);
  const ticks = 36 + ((h >> 3) % 36);
  const mono = label.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).map((w) => w[0]).join("").slice(0, 2).toUpperCase();
  return (
    <svg width={size} height={size} viewBox="0 0 100 100" data-testid={testid} aria-label={`Edition seal ${mono}`} className="shrink-0">
      <circle cx="50" cy="50" r="48" fill="none" stroke={color} strokeWidth="0.8" />
      {Array.from({ length: ticks }, (_, i) => {
        const a = (i / ticks) * Math.PI * 2;
        const r2 = i % 3 ? 41 : 37;
        return <line key={i} x1={50 + 44 * Math.cos(a)} y1={50 + 44 * Math.sin(a)} x2={50 + r2 * Math.cos(a)} y2={50 + r2 * Math.sin(a)} stroke={color} strokeWidth="0.6" />;
      })}
      <g transform={`rotate(${h % 360} 50 50)`}>
        {Array.from({ length: petals }, (_, i) => (
          <ellipse key={i} cx="50" cy="50" rx="31" ry={7 + ((h >> (i % 8)) % 11)} fill="none" stroke={color} strokeWidth="0.5" opacity="0.75" transform={`rotate(${(180 / petals) * i} 50 50)`} />
        ))}
      </g>
      <circle cx="50" cy="50" r="15" fill="#070707" stroke={color} strokeWidth="0.8" />
      <text x="50" y="55" textAnchor="middle" fontFamily="Cormorant Garamond, serif" fontSize="14" fill={color}>{mono}</text>
    </svg>
  );
}
```

## 35. src/components/os/TemplateFrame.jsx

```jsx
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { TokensProvider, useTokens } from "@/context/TokensContext";
import { templateComponent } from "@/components/templates";
import { MOTION_CLASS, toCssVars } from "@/lib/tokens";

const WIDTHS = { desktop: 1440, tablet: 834, phone: 390 };

// Renders a template at its real canvas width, scaled to whatever box it sits
// in, so a phone preview is a phone and a desktop preview is a desktop.
export default function TemplateFrame({ slug, tokens, device = "desktop", captureRef, preview = false, testid }) {
  const existing = useTokens();
  const Tpl = templateComponent(slug);
  const width = WIDTHS[device] || 1440;
  const wrap = useRef(null);
  const inner = useRef(null);
  const [boxWidth, setBoxWidth] = useState(0);
  const [docHeight, setDocHeight] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!wrap.current) return undefined;
    const ro = new ResizeObserver(([e]) => setBoxWidth(e.contentRect.width));
    ro.observe(wrap.current);
    return () => ro.disconnect();
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!inner.current) return undefined;
    const ro = new ResizeObserver(([e]) => setDocHeight(e.contentRect.height));
    ro.observe(inner.current);
    return () => ro.disconnect();
  }, [slug, device]);

  const scale = boxWidth ? boxWidth / width : 0.4;
  const vars = toCssVars(tokens || {});
  const body = Tpl ? <Tpl /> : <div className="p-16">This template is not in the catalogue.</div>;

  return (
    <div ref={wrap} className="relative h-full overflow-y-auto overflow-x-hidden no-scrollbar bg-[#0B0B0A]" data-testid={testid}>
      <div style={{ height: docHeight ? docHeight * scale : 600, position: "relative" }}>
        <div style={{ width, position: "absolute", top: 0, left: 0, transform: `scale(${scale})`, transformOrigin: "0 0" }}>
          <div ref={inner} className={`tpl-scope ${MOTION_CLASS(tokens?.motion)}`} style={vars}>
            <div ref={captureRef}>
              {existing ? (
                body
              ) : (
                <TokensProvider key={slug} slug={slug} initial={tokens} preview={preview}>
                  {body}
                </TokensProvider>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## 36. src/components/templates/CgCart.jsx

```jsx
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Search, ShoppingBasket, Star, Plus, Zap } from "lucide-react";
import { Reveal, useImgs, Photo } from "./kit";

const PRODUCTS = [
  ["Everyday Mug 12oz", 8.99, 14.99, 4.8, "mugWhite"],
  ["Studio Tote, Yellow", 24.0, 39.0, 4.6, "bagYellow"],
  ["Eau de Bleu 50ml", 29.5, 45.0, 4.7, "perfumeBlue"],
  ["Pantry Oil Duo", 15.49, 21.0, 4.9, "pantry"],
  ["Plain Mug 2-pack", 11.99, 18.0, 4.5, "mugPlain"],
  ["Rose Mist 30ml", 19.0, 32.0, 4.4, "perfumePink"],
  ["Gilded Cup", 13.0, 22.0, 4.8, "mugGold"],
  ["Sauce Sampler x6", 17.99, 26.0, 4.7, "condiments"],
];

function Countdown() {
  const [s, setS] = useState(5 * 3600 + 42 * 60 + 17);
  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setInterval(() => setS((x) => (x > 0 ? x - 1 : 0)), 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(t);
  }, []);
  const f = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, "0");
  return (
    <div className="flex gap-2 t-mono" data-testid="cart-countdown">
      {[Math.floor(s / 3600), Math.floor((s % 3600) / 60), s % 60].map((v, i) => (
        <span key={i} className="t-bg-ink t-r-sm px-3 py-2 text-2xl font-bold">{f(v)}</span>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

export default function CgCart() {
  const I = useImgs();
  const [basket, setBasket] = useState([]);
  const [bump, setBump] = useState(0);
  const add = (p) => {
    setBasket((b) => [...b, p]);
    setBump((n) => n + 1);
  };
  const total = basket.reduce((s, p) => s + p[1], 0);
  return (
    <div className="relative pb-28 t-bg">
      <div className="t-bg-accent overflow-hidden py-2 text-sm font-semibold">
        <div className="t-marquee gap-12">
          {[...Array(4)].flatMap((_, k) =>
            ["Free delivery over $25", "2-hour slots near you", "Weekend mega drop −40%", "Easy 30-day returns"].map((t) => (
              <span key={t + k} className="px-6">{t}</span>
            ))
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
      <nav className="sticky top-0 z-20 t-bg flex items-center gap-4 px-6 py-4 border-b t-line">
        <div className="t-head text-3xl font-extrabold tracking-tighter t-primary">snapcart</div>
        <div className="hidden sm:flex flex-1 items-center gap-2 t-surface t-r px-4 py-3 min-w-0">
          <Search className="h-5 w-5 t-muted shrink-0" />
          <span className="t-muted truncate">Search mugs, bags, gifts…</span>
        </div>
        <Search className="sm:hidden ml-auto h-6 w-6" />
        <button data-testid="cart-basket-button" className="t-btn relative">
          <ShoppingBasket className="h-5 w-5" /><span className="hidden sm:inline">Basket</span>
          <span key={bump} className="rise absolute -top-2 -right-2 h-6 min-w-6 px-1 rounded-full t-bg-accent text-xs font-bold grid place-items-center" data-testid="cart-count">{basket.length}</span>
        </button>
      </nav>
      <section className="grid lg:grid-cols-3 t-gap p-6">
        <Reveal className="lg:col-span-2 t-bg-primary t-r t-pad relative overflow-hidden min-h-[320px] flex flex-col justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 font-semibold"><Zap className="h-5 w-5" />Weekend mega drop</div>
          <h1 className="t-head text-5xl lg:text-7xl font-extrabold tracking-tighter leading-[0.9]">Up to 40% off<br />everyday favourites.</h1>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-6">
            <Countdown />
            <button className="t-btn" style={{ background: "var(--t-ink)", color: "var(--t-bg)" }}>Shop the drop</button>
          </div>
          <Photo src={I.bagYellow} alt="" className="hidden md:block absolute right-6 bottom-6 w-56 h-56 object-cover t-r rotate-6 t-float" />
        </Reveal>
        <div className="grid t-gap">
          <div className="t-surface t-r t-pad">
            <div className="t-head text-3xl font-extrabold">$5 off</div>
            <p className="t-muted text-sm">your first order with code SNAP5</p>
          </div>
          <div className="t-bg-accent t-r t-pad">
            <div className="t-head text-3xl font-extrabold">2-hour</div>
            <p className="text-sm">delivery slots in 40 cities</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
      <div className="flex gap-3 px-6 overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar">
        {["Deals", "Kitchen", "Bags", "Beauty", "Pantry", "Gifts under $20", "New in"].map((c, i) => (
          <span key={c} className={`px-5 py-2 rounded-full whitespace-nowrap font-semibold text-sm t-lift ${i === 0 ? "t-bg-ink" : "t-surface"}`}>{c}</span>
        ))}
      </div>
      <section className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 t-gap p-6">
        {PRODUCTS.map((p, i) => (
          <Reveal key={p[0]} delay={i * 0.04} className="group">
            <div className="t-surface t-r overflow-hidden t-zoom relative aspect-square">
              <Photo src={I[p[4]]} alt={p[0]} className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
              <span className="absolute top-3 left-3 t-bg-primary rounded-full px-2 py-1 text-xs font-bold">−{Math.round((1 - p[1] / p[2]) * 100)}%</span>
              <button data-testid={`cart-add-${i}`} onClick={() => add(p)} className="absolute bottom-3 right-3 t-btn !p-3 rounded-full"><Plus className="h-5 w-5" /></button>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-3 font-semibold">{p[0]}</div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
              <span className="font-bold text-lg">${p[1].toFixed(2)}</span>
              <span className="t-muted line-through">${p[2].toFixed(2)}</span>
              <span className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-1 t-muted"><Star className="h-3 w-3 fill-current" />{p[3]}</span>
            </div>
          </Reveal>
        ))}
      </section>
      {basket.length > 0 && (
        <div className="sticky bottom-4 mx-6 t-bg-ink t-r t-pad-sm flex items-center justify-between rise" data-testid="cart-sticky-bar">
          <span className="font-semibold">
            {basket.length} item{basket.length > 1 ? "s" : ""} · <b data-testid="cart-total">${total.toFixed(2)}</b>
            {total < 25 && <span className="opacity-60"> · ${(25 - total).toFixed(2)} to free delivery</span>}
          </span>
          <button className="t-btn">Checkout</button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```jsx
      <footer className="t-sec border-t t-line flex flex-wrap justify-between gap-6 items-end">
        <div className="t-head text-5xl italic">Maison Vey</div>
        <Label className="t-muted">Private appointments · Paris · Milano · London · No. 012/250</Label>
```

## 38. src/components/templates/CgWholesale.jsx

```jsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { Search, ShoppingCart, Truck, Minus, Plus, RotateCcw, BadgeCheck } from "lucide-react";
import { Reveal, Label, useImgs, Photo } from "./kit";

const ROWS = [
  { sku: "QM-1042", name: "Cold-pressed olive oil 500ml", pack: 12, p: [96, 88, 79] },
  { sku: "QM-2210", name: "Stoneground mustard 250g", pack: 24, p: [84, 76, 68] },
  { sku: "QM-3301", name: "Sea salt flakes 125g tin", pack: 36, p: [118, 106, 94] },
  { sku: "QM-4180", name: "Aged balsamic 250ml", pack: 6, p: [72, 66, 58] },
  { sku: "QM-5077", name: "Chili crisp jar 180g", pack: 18, p: [99, 90, 81] },
];
const BREAKS = ["1–9 cases", "10–49", "50+"];
const tierIdx = (q) => (q >= 50 ? 2 : q >= 10 ? 1 : 0);

function OrderMatrix() {
  const [qty, setQty] = useState({ "QM-1042": 12, "QM-3301": 4 });
  const set = (sku, v) => setQty((q) => ({ ...q, [sku]: Math.max(0, v) }));
  const total = ROWS.reduce((s, r) => s + (qty[r.sku] || 0) * r.p[tierIdx(qty[r.sku] || 0)], 0);
  const cases = Object.values(qty).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
  return (
    <section className="t-sec border-t t-line">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <Label className="t-primary">Bulk order matrix</Label>
          <h2 className="t-head text-4xl font-bold tracking-tight mt-3">Build the order. Pricing breaks apply live.</h2>
        </div>
        <button className="t-btn t-btn-ghost text-sm"><RotateCcw className="h-4 w-4" />Repeat order #8812</button>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-10 border t-line t-r overflow-x-auto t-surface">
        <table className="w-full text-sm min-w-[820px]">
          <thead>
            <tr className="border-b t-line text-left t-muted">
              <th className="t-pad-sm font-normal"><Label>SKU</Label></th>
              <th className="t-pad-sm font-normal"><Label>Product</Label></th>
              <th className="t-pad-sm font-normal"><Label>Case</Label></th>
              {BREAKS.map((b) => <th key={b} className="t-pad-sm font-normal text-right"><Label>{b}</Label></th>)}
              <th className="t-pad-sm font-normal text-center"><Label>Cases</Label></th>
              <th className="t-pad-sm font-normal text-right"><Label>Line</Label></th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {ROWS.map((r) => {
              const q = qty[r.sku] || 0;
              const ti = tierIdx(q);
              return (
                <tr key={r.sku} className="border-b t-line last:border-0" data-testid={`wholesale-row-${r.sku}`}>
                  <td className="t-pad-sm t-mono t-muted">{r.sku}</td>
                  <td className="t-pad-sm font-medium">{r.name}</td>
                  <td className="t-pad-sm t-muted">×{r.pack}</td>
                  {r.p.map((p, i) => (
                    <td key={i} className={`t-pad-sm text-right t-mono ${q && i === ti ? "t-primary font-bold" : "t-muted"}`}>${p}</td>
                  ))}
                  <td className="t-pad-sm">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-center border t-line t-r-sm w-fit mx-auto">
                      <button data-testid={`wholesale-dec-${r.sku}`} className="p-2" onClick={() => set(r.sku, q - 1)}><Minus className="h-3 w-3" /></button>
                      <input data-testid={`wholesale-qty-${r.sku}`} value={q} onChange={(e) => set(r.sku, parseInt(e.target.value || "0", 10) || 0)} className="w-12 text-center bg-transparent t-mono outline-none" />
                      <button data-testid={`wholesale-inc-${r.sku}`} className="p-2" onClick={() => set(r.sku, q + 1)}><Plus className="h-3 w-3" /></button>
                    </div>
                  </td>
                  <td className="t-pad-sm text-right t-mono font-bold">${(q * r.p[ti]).toLocaleString()}</td>
                </tr>
              );
            })}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 mt-6 t-bg-ink t-r t-pad">
        <div className="flex gap-10">
          <div><Label className="opacity-60">Cases</Label><div className="t-head text-3xl font-bold" data-testid="wholesale-cases">{cases}</div></div>
          <div><Label className="opacity-60">Order total (ex. VAT)</Label><div className="t-head text-3xl font-bold" data-testid="wholesale-total">${total.toLocaleString()}</div></div>
          <div><Label className="opacity-60">Freight</Label><div className="t-head text-3xl font-bold">{total >= 1500 ? "Free" : `$${(1500 - total).toLocaleString()} to free`}</div></div>
        </div>
        <button className="t-btn" data-testid="wholesale-submit" onClick={() => toast.success(`PO drafted · ${cases} cases · $${total.toLocaleString()} on net-30`)}>Submit PO on net-30</button>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

export default function CgWholesale() {
  const I = useImgs();
  return (
    <div className="t-bg">
```
