## 103. src/lib/AuthContext.jsx

```jsx
import React, { createContext, useState, useContext, useEffect } from 'react';
import { base44 } from '@/api/base44Client';
import { appParams } from '@/lib/app-params';

const AuthContext = createContext();

export const AuthProvider = ({ children }) => {
  const [user, setUser] = useState(null);
  const [isAuthenticated, setIsAuthenticated] = useState(false);
  const [isLoadingAuth, setIsLoadingAuth] = useState(true);
  const [isLoadingPublicSettings, setIsLoadingPublicSettings] = useState(true);
  const [authError, setAuthError] = useState(null);
  const [authChecked, setAuthChecked] = useState(false);
  const [appPublicSettings, setAppPublicSettings] = useState(null); // Contains only { id, public_settings }

  useEffect(() => {
    checkAppState();
  }, []);

  const checkAppState = async () => {
    try {
      setIsLoadingPublicSettings(true);
      setAuthError(null);
      
      try {
        const publicSettings = await base44.app.getPublicSettings();
        setAppPublicSettings(publicSettings);
        
        // If we got the app public settings successfully, check if user is authenticated
        if (appParams.token) {
          await checkUserAuth();
        } else {
          setIsLoadingAuth(false);
          setIsAuthenticated(false);
          setAuthChecked(true);
        }
        setIsLoadingPublicSettings(false);
      } catch (appError) {
        console.error('App state check failed:', appError);
        
        // Handle app-level errors
        if (appError.status === 403 && appError.data?.extra_data?.reason) {
          const reason = appError.data.extra_data.reason;
          if (reason === 'auth_required') {
            setAuthError({
              type: 'auth_required',
              message: 'Authentication required'
            });
          } else if (reason === 'user_not_registered') {
            setAuthError({
              type: 'user_not_registered',
              message: 'User not registered for this app'
            });
          } else {
            setAuthError({
              type: reason,
              message: appError.message
            });
          }
        } else {
          setAuthError({
            type: 'unknown',
            message: appError.message || 'Failed to load app'
          });
        }
        setIsLoadingPublicSettings(false);
        setIsLoadingAuth(false);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Unexpected error:', error);
      setAuthError({
        type: 'unknown',
        message: error.message || 'An unexpected error occurred'
      });
      setIsLoadingPublicSettings(false);
      setIsLoadingAuth(false);
    }
  };

  const checkUserAuth = async () => {
    try {
      // Now check if the user is authenticated
      setIsLoadingAuth(true);
      const currentUser = await base44.auth.me();
      setUser(currentUser);
      setIsAuthenticated(true);
      setIsLoadingAuth(false);
      setAuthChecked(true);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('User auth check failed:', error);
      setIsLoadingAuth(false);
      setIsAuthenticated(false);
      setAuthChecked(true);
      
      // If user auth fails, it might be an expired token
      if (error.status === 401 || error.status === 403) {
        setAuthError({
          type: 'auth_required',
          message: 'Authentication required'
        });
      }
    }
  };

  const logout = (shouldRedirect = true) => {
    setUser(null);
    setIsAuthenticated(false);
    
    if (shouldRedirect) {
      // Use the SDK's logout method which handles token cleanup and redirect
      base44.auth.logout(window.location.href);
    } else {
      // Just remove the token without redirect
      base44.auth.logout();
    }
  };

  const navigateToLogin = () => {
    // Use the SDK's redirectToLogin method
    base44.auth.redirectToLogin(window.location.href);
  };

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider value={{ 
      user, 
      isAuthenticated, 
      isLoadingAuth,
      isLoadingPublicSettings,
      authError,
      appPublicSettings,
      authChecked,
      logout,
      navigateToLogin,
      checkUserAuth,
      checkAppState
    }}>
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
};

export const useAuth = () => {
  const context = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useAuth must be used within an AuthProvider');
  }
  return context;
};
```

## 104. src/lib/PageNotFound.jsx

```jsx
import { useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
import { base44 } from '@/api/base44Client';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';


export default function PageNotFound({}) {
    const location = useLocation();
    const pageName = location.pathname.substring(1);

    const { data: authData, isFetched } = useQuery({
        queryKey: ['user'],
        queryFn: async () => {
            try {
                const user = await base44.auth.me();
                return { user, isAuthenticated: true };
            } catch (error) {
                return { user: null, isAuthenticated: false };
            }
        }
    });
    
    return (
        <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center p-6 bg-slate-50">
            <div className="max-w-md w-full">
                <div className="text-center space-y-6">
                    {/* 404 Error Code */}
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                        <h1 className="text-7xl font-light text-slate-300">404</h1>
                        <div className="h-0.5 w-16 bg-slate-200 mx-auto"></div>
                    </div>
                    
                    {/* Main Message */}
                    <div className="space-y-3">
                        <h2 className="text-2xl font-medium text-slate-800">
                            Page Not Found
                        </h2>
                        <p className="text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">
                            The page <span className="font-medium text-slate-700">"{pageName}"</span> could not be found in this application.
                        </p>
                    </div>
                    
                    {/* Admin Note */}
                    {isFetched && authData.isAuthenticated && authData.user?.role === 'admin' && (
                        <div className="mt-8 p-4 bg-slate-100 rounded-lg border border-slate-200">
                            <div className="flex items-start space-x-3">
                                <div className="flex-shrink-0 w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-orange-100 flex items-center justify-center mt-0.5">
                                    <div className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-orange-400"></div>
                                </div>
                                <div className="text-left space-y-1">
                                    <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Admin Note</p>
                                    <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">
                                        This could mean that the AI hasn't implemented this page yet. Ask it to implement it in the chat.
                                    </p>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    )}
                    
                    {/* Action Button */}
                    <div className="pt-6">
                        <button 
                            onClick={() => window.location.href = '/'} 
                            className="inline-flex items-center px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-50 hover:border-slate-300 transition-colors duration-200 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-slate-500"
                        >
                            <svg className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M3 12l2-2m0 0l7-7 7 7M5 10v10a1 1 0 001 1h3m10-11l2 2m-2-2v10a1 1 0 01-1 1h-3m-6 0a1 1 0 001-1v-4a1 1 0 011-1h2a1 1 0 011 1v4a1 1 0 001 1m-6 0h6" />
                            </svg>
                            Go Home
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    )
}
```

## 105. src/lib/app-params.js

```js
import { getAccessToken } from '@base44/sdk';

const isNode = typeof window === 'undefined';

const isClearAccessTokenRequested = () =>
	!isNode && new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("clear_access_token") === 'true';

const clearStoredAccessToken = () => {
	window.localStorage.removeItem('base44_access_token');
	window.localStorage.removeItem('token');
}

const getAppParams = () => {
	if (isClearAccessTokenRequested()) {
		clearStoredAccessToken();
	}
	return {
		appId: import.meta.env.VITE_BASE44_APP_ID,
		token: getAccessToken(),
		functionsVersion: (!isNode && new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("functions_version")?.trim()) || import.meta.env.VITE_BASE44_FUNCTIONS_VERSION,
		appBaseUrl: import.meta.env.VITE_BASE44_APP_BASE_URL,
	}
}


export const appParams = {
	...getAppParams()
}
```

## 106. src/lib/authReturnTo.js

```js
// Shared by the auth pages (Login, Register, and any page that resumes a flow
// after sign-in, e.g. the MCP OAuth consent page). Keep the redirect
// validation in one place — it is security-sensitive and easy to drift.

// Resolve ?returnTo= to a safe same-origin path, else "/".
//
// The same-origin check alone is not enough: a value like /.//evil.com or
// /\evil.com parses same-origin but normalizes to a protocol-relative
// //evil.com when assigned to location.href — an open redirect. So require the
// resolved path to be exactly one leading slash (no "//" prefix, no backslash).
export function safeReturnTo() {
  const raw = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("returnTo");
  if (!raw) return "/";
  try {
    const url = new URL(raw, window.location.origin);
    if (url.origin !== window.location.origin) return "/";
    // Only access_token/clear_access_token are still URL-read by app-params.js, but the
    // whole bootstrap set stays stripped: one going back to a URL read must not silently
    // become injectable again. Normal app-flow params (e.g. the OAuth consent ctx) are kept.
    for (const p of ["access_token", "clear_access_token", "app_id", "app_base_url", "functions_version", "from_url"]) {
      url.searchParams.delete(p);
    }
    const path = url.pathname + url.search;
    if (!path.startsWith("/") || path.startsWith("//") || path.includes("\\")) return "/";
    return path;
  } catch {
    return "/";
  }
}
```

```js
export const templateBySlug = (slug) => CATALOG.find((t) => t.slug === slug);

export const editionLabel = (slug) => {
  const t = templateBySlug(slug);
  if (!t) return "Edition —";
  return `No. ${String(t.edition.no).padStart(3, "0")} / ${t.edition.of}`;
};
```

## 108. src/lib/exportSite.js

```js
import { toCssVars } from "@/lib/tokens";
import { editionLabel } from "@/lib/catalog";

const FONT_IMPORT =
  "@import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Cormorant+Garamond:ital,wght@0,300;0,400;0,500;0,600;1,300;1,400;1,500&family=Inter:wght@300;400;500;600;700&family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;500&family=Space+Grotesk:wght@400;500;600;700&family=Sora:wght@400;600;700;800&family=Manrope:wght@300;400;500;600;700&family=Playfair+Display:ital,wght@0,400;0,600;0,800;1,400;1,600&family=Fraunces:ital,opsz,wght@0,9..144,400;0,9..144,600;0,9..144,700;1,9..144,400&display=swap');";

// The OS renders in Tailwind, so the stylesheet of the running page is the
// template's stylesheet. Collect it, inline it with the tokens, and the export
// is one file that opens anywhere.
const collectCss = () =>
  Array.from(document.styleSheets)
    .map((sheet) => {
      try {
        return Array.from(sheet.cssRules).map((rule) => rule.cssText).join("\n");
      } catch {
        return "";
      }
    })
    .join("\n");

const varBlock = (tokens) =>
  Object.entries(toCssVars(tokens))
    .map(([k, v]) => `${k}:${v}`)
    .join(";");

export const buildSiteHtml = (node, tokens, project) => {
  const name = project?.name || "OmniForge direction";
  return `<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8" />
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1" />
<title>${name} — OmniForge</title>
<meta name="generator" content="OmniForge Template OS" />
<meta name="description" content="${(project?.family || "omniforge")} template export · ${editionLabel(project?.template_slug)}" />
<style>${FONT_IMPORT}
${collectCss()}
html,body{margin:0;padding:0}
.tpl-scope{${varBlock(tokens)}}
</style>
</head>
<body>
<!-- ${name} · ${editionLabel(project?.template_slug)} · exported from OmniForge Template OS -->
<div class="tpl-scope t-bg" data-omniforge="${project?.template_slug || ""}">
${node.innerHTML}
</div>
</body>
</html>`;
};

const slugify = (s) => String(s || "omniforge").replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/gi, "-").replace(/^-|-$/g, "").toLowerCase();

export const downloadSite = (node, tokens, project) => {
  const html = buildSiteHtml(node, tokens, project);
  const blob = new Blob([html], { type: "text/html;charset=utf-8" });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement("a");
  a.href = url;
  a.download = `${slugify(project?.name)}-${project?.template_slug || "site"}.html`;
  document.body.appendChild(a);
  a.click();
  a.remove();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  return html.length;
};

export const downloadText = (text, filename) => {
  const blob = new Blob([text], { type: "text/markdown;charset=utf-8" });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement("a");
  a.href = url;
  a.download = filename;
  document.body.appendChild(a);
  a.click();
  a.remove();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
};
```

## 111. src/lib/store.js

```js
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";

/* ---------- share codes ---------- */

const ALPHABET = "ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789";

export const shareCode = () => {
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(10);
  crypto.getRandomValues(bytes);
  let out = "";
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += 1) out += ALPHABET[bytes[i] % ALPHABET.length];
  return out;
};

/* ---------- who am I ---------- */

let mePromise = null;

// Share links have to be readable by whoever holds the code, so project reads
// stay open and every owner-scoped query says out loud whose projects it wants.
export const currentUserId = () => {
  if (!mePromise) {
    mePromise = base44.auth
      .me()
      .then((u) => u?.id || null)
      .catch(() => null);
  }
  return mePromise;
};

/* ---------- projects ---------- */

export const listProjects = async () => {
  const uid = await currentUserId();
  const page = await base44.entities.Project.filter(uid ? { created_by_id: uid } : {}, { sort: "-created_date", limit: 100 });
  return page.items;
};

export const listProjectsPage = async (limit = 12, cursor) => {
  const uid = await currentUserId();
  return base44.entities.Project.filter(uid ? { created_by_id: uid } : {}, { sort: "-created_date", limit, cursor });
};

export const getProject = async (id) => {
  const uid = await currentUserId();
  const project = await base44.entities.Project.get(id).catch((e) => (e.status === 404 ? null : Promise.reject(e)));
  if (!project) return null;
  return !uid || project.created_by_id === uid ? project : null;
};

export const projectByCode = async (code) => {
  if (!code) return null;
  const page = await base44.entities.Project.filter({ share_code: code }, { limit: 1 });
  return (page.items || [])[0] || null;
};

export const createProject = (data) =>
  base44.entities.Project.create({ status: "draft", share_code: shareCode(), ...data });

export const updateProject = (id, data) => base44.entities.Project.update(id, data);

export const deleteProject = (id) => base44.entities.Project.delete(id);

export const countProjects = async () => {
  const uid = await currentUserId();
  return base44.entities.Project.count(uid ? { created_by_id: uid } : {});
};

export const countApproved = async () => {
  const uid = await currentUserId();
  return base44.entities.Project.count(uid ? { created_by_id: uid, status: "approved" } : { status: "approved" });
};

/* ---------- reusable themes ---------- */

export const listThemes = (slug) =>
  base44.entities.Theme.filter({ template_slug: slug }, { sort: "-created_date", limit: 50 }).then((p) => p.items);

export const createTheme = (data) => base44.entities.Theme.create(data);
export const deleteTheme = (id) => base44.entities.Theme.delete(id);

/* ---------- client review ---------- */

export const listComments = (projectId) =>
  base44.entities.ProjectComment.filter({ project_id: projectId }, { sort: "created_date", limit: 200 }).then((p) => p.items);

export const addComment = (data) => base44.entities.ProjectComment.create(data);
export const setCommentResolved = (id, resolved) => base44.entities.ProjectComment.update(id, { resolved });
export const deleteComment = (id) => base44.entities.ProjectComment.delete(id);

/* ---------- briefs and assets ---------- */

export const listBriefs = () => base44.entities.Brief.filter({}, { sort: "-created_date", limit: 24 }).then((p) => p.items);
export const getBrief = (id) => base44.entities.Brief.get(id);
export const createBrief = (data) => base44.entities.Brief.create(data);
export const updateBrief = (id, data) => base44.entities.Brief.update(id, data);
export const deleteBrief = (id) => base44.entities.Brief.delete(id);

export const listAssets = () => base44.entities.Asset.filter({}, { sort: "-created_date", limit: 60 }).then((p) => p.items);
export const createAsset = (data) => base44.entities.Asset.create(data);
export const deleteAsset = (id) => base44.entities.Asset.delete(id);

export const countAssets = () => base44.entities.Asset.count({});
```

## 112. src/lib/tokens.js

```js
// OmniForge — token engine.
// Every template in the OS is rendered from a token set. Nothing about a
// template's colour, type, geometry, rhythm or motion is hard-coded in its JSX.

export const FONT_PAIRS = [
  { id: "editorial", name: "Editorial", head: "'Cormorant Garamond', Georgia, serif", body: "'Inter', system-ui, sans-serif", mono: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace" },
  { id: "modern", name: "Modern", head: "'Inter', system-ui, sans-serif", body: "'Inter', system-ui, sans-serif", mono: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace" },
  { id: "grotesk", name: "Grotesk", head: "'Space Grotesk', system-ui, sans-serif", body: "'Inter', system-ui, sans-serif", mono: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace" },
  { id: "contrast", name: "Contrast", head: "'Playfair Display', Georgia, serif", body: "'Manrope', system-ui, sans-serif", mono: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace" },
  { id: "geometric", name: "Geometric", head: "'Sora', system-ui, sans-serif", body: "'Inter', system-ui, sans-serif", mono: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace" },
  { id: "soft", name: "Soft", head: "'Fraunces', Georgia, serif", body: "'Manrope', system-ui, sans-serif", mono: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace" },
  { id: "technical", name: "Technical", head: "'Space Grotesk', system-ui, sans-serif", body: "'Inter', system-ui, sans-serif", mono: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace" },
];

export const MOTION = {
  0: { label: "Still", dur: 0, lift: 0 },
  1: { label: "Subtle", dur: 180, lift: 1 },
  2: { label: "Measured", dur: 320, lift: 1.6 },
  3: { label: "Expressive", dur: 520, lift: 2.2 },
};

export const COLOR_KEYS = [
  ["bg", "Background"],
  ["surface", "Surface"],
  ["ink", "Ink"],
  ["muted", "Muted text"],
  ["primary", "Primary"],
  ["onPrimary", "Label on primary"],
  ["accent", "Accent"],
  ["onAccent", "Label on accent"],
];

export const ACCENT_SWATCHES = [
  "#D4AF37", "#C9A227", "#4F46E5", "#2563EB", "#0E9F6E", "#1F5F43",
  "#FF5A1F", "#E23744", "#B4551F", "#7C3AED", "#111111", "#F0B429",
];

export const fontPair = (id) => FONT_PAIRS.find((f) => f.id === id) || FONT_PAIRS[1];

/* ---------- colour maths ---------- */

const clamp255 = (n) => Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round(n)));

export const hexToRgb = (hex) => {
  const h = String(hex || "").trim().replace("#", "");
  const full = h.length === 3 ? h.split("").map((c) => c + c).join("") : h;
  const n = parseInt(full || "000000", 16);
  return { r: (n >> 16) & 255, g: (n >> 8) & 255, b: n & 255 };
};

export const isHex = (v) => typeof v === "string" && /^#([0-9a-f]{3}|[0-9a-f]{6})$/i.test(v.trim());

export const rgba = (hex, a = 1) => {
  const { r, g, b } = hexToRgb(hex);
  return `rgba(${r},${g},${b},${a})`;
};

export const mix = (a, b, t = 0.5) => {
  const x = hexToRgb(a);
  const y = hexToRgb(b);
  return `#${[0, 1, 2]
    .map((i) => clamp255(x[["r", "g", "b"][i]] * (1 - t) + y[["r", "g", "b"][i]] * t).toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
    .join("")}`;
};

const luminance = (hex) => {
  const { r, g, b } = hexToRgb(hex);
  const chan = (c) => {
    const v = c / 255;
    return v <= 0.03928 ? v / 12.92 : Math.pow((v + 0.055) / 1.055, 2.4);
  };
  return 0.2126 * chan(r) + 0.7152 * chan(g) + 0.0722 * chan(b);
};

export const contrast = (a, b) => {
  const l1 = luminance(a);
  const l2 = luminance(b);
  const hi = Math.max(l1, l2);
  const lo = Math.min(l1, l2);
  return Math.round(((hi + 0.05) / (lo + 0.05)) * 100) / 100;
};

// Re-derive readable ink from a background rather than trusting a stored one.
export const readableOn = (bg) => (contrast("#0B0B0A", bg) >= contrast("#F7F4EC", bg) ? "#0B0B0A" : "#F7F4EC");

/* ---------- token sets ---------- */

const T = (defaults, presets) => ({ defaults, presets });

/* ---------- CSS variables ---------- */

export const toCssVars = (t) => {
  const f = fontPair(t.font);
  return {
    "--t-bg": t.bg,
    "--t-ink": t.ink,
    "--t-surface": t.surface,
    "--t-muted": t.muted,
    "--t-primary": t.primary,
    "--t-on-primary": t.onPrimary,
    "--t-accent": t.accent,
    "--t-on-accent": t.onAccent,
    "--t-line": rgba(t.ink, 0.16),
    "--t-radius": `${Number(t.radius) || 0}px`,
    "--t-space": String(t.space ?? 1),
    "--t-head": f.head,
    "--t-body": f.body,
    "--t-mono": f.mono,
    "--t-motion": `${MOTION[t.motion]?.dur ?? 0}ms`,
  };
};

export const MOTION_CLASS = (motion) => (MOTION[motion]?.dur ? "" : "tpl-still");

/* ---------- prestige score (the export gate) ---------- */

export const prestigeScore = (t) => {
  const checks = [
    { label: "Body ink on background", ratio: contrast(t.ink, t.bg), target: 7 },
    { label: "Muted text on background", ratio: contrast(t.muted, t.bg), target: 4.5 },
    { label: "Accent on background", ratio: contrast(t.accent, t.bg), target: 3 },
    { label: "Label on primary", ratio: contrast(t.onPrimary, t.primary), target: 4.5 },
    { label: "Surface separation", ratio: contrast(t.surface, t.bg), target: 1.05 },
  ].map((c) => ({ ...c, pass: c.ratio >= c.target }));
  const score = Math.round((checks.reduce((s, c) => s + Math.min(1, c.ratio / c.target), 0) / checks.length) * 100);
  const grade = score >= 97 ? "A+" : score >= 92 ? "A" : score >= 85 ? "B" : score >= 76 ? "C" : "D";
  return { score, grade, checks, gate: 85 };
};

/* ---------- brief tokens ---------- */

const num = (v, fallback) => (typeof v === "number" && !Number.isNaN(v) ? v : fallback);

// A brief supplies a palette; readability is always re-derived from that palette
// so a dark brief can never inherit a light template's ink (or the reverse).
export const sanitizeBriefTokens = (raw = {}, slug) => {
  const base = TEMPLATE_TOKENS[slug]?.defaults || TEMPLATE_TOKENS["saas-orbit"].defaults;
  const bg = isHex(raw.bg) ? raw.bg : base.bg;
  const primary = isHex(raw.primary) ? raw.primary : base.primary;
  const accent = isHex(raw.accent) ? raw.accent : base.accent;
  const ink = readableOn(bg);
  return {
    ...base,
    bg,
    primary,
    accent,
    ink,
    surface: isHex(raw.surface) ? raw.surface : mix(ink, bg, 0.94),
    muted: isHex(raw.muted) ? raw.muted : mix(ink, bg, 0.45),
    onPrimary: readableOn(primary),
    onAccent: readableOn(accent),
    radius: num(raw.radius, base.radius),
    space: num(raw.space, base.space),
    motion: num(raw.motion, base.motion),
    font: FONT_PAIRS.some((f) => f.id === raw.font) ? raw.font : base.font,
    images: raw.images || {},
  };
};
```

## 115. src/pages/AssetStudio.jsx

```jsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { ImagePlus, Trash2, ExternalLink, Copy } from "lucide-react";
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";
import { PageHead } from "@/components/os/Shell";
import { Image } from "@/components/ui/image";
import { createAsset, deleteAsset, listAssets } from "@/lib/store";

const IDEAS = [
  "A single ceramic mug on a linen cloth, morning light from the left",
  "A wide shot of a warehouse aisle at dawn, pallets stacked to the ceiling",
  "A dark still life of a perfume flacon on wet stone",
  "A dining room with one long oak table, candles just lit",
];

export default function AssetStudio() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const [prompt, setPrompt] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const { data: assets = [] } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["assets"], queryFn: listAssets });

  const generate = async () => {
    if (prompt.trim().length < 6) return toast.error("Describe the image first.");
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const res = await base44.functions.invoke("generateImages", { kind: "asset", prompt: prompt.trim() });
      const data = res.data || {};
      if (data.error) throw new Error(data.error);
      await createAsset({ prompt: data.prompt || prompt.trim(), url: data.url, kind: "studio" });
      setPrompt("");
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["assets"] });
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["ledger"] });
      toast.success("Image generated — it is now available to every image slot");
    } catch (e) {
      toast.error(e.message || "The studio could not generate that image.");
    }
    setBusy(false);
  };

  const remove = async (a) => {
    await deleteAsset(a.id);
    qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["assets"] });
    qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["ledger"] });
  };

  const copy = (url) => navigator.clipboard?.writeText(url).then(() => toast.success("Image URL copied"));

  return (
    <div>
      <PageHead no="09" eyebrow="AI image studio" title="Imagery that fits the palette">
        <div className="text-right">
          <div className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F]">In the bank</div>
          <div className="font-display text-3xl mt-1">{assets.length}</div>
        </div>
      </PageHead>

      <div className="px-6 md:px-14 py-10 grid lg:grid-cols-12 gap-10">
        <div className="lg:col-span-4">
          <div className="bg-[#0B0B0A] border border-white/[0.08] p-6">
            <div className="eyebrow text-[#D4AF37]">Describe the shot</div>
            <textarea
              data-testid="asset-prompt"
              value={prompt}
              onChange={(e) => setPrompt(e.target.value)}
              rows={4}
              placeholder="A single stoneware mug on a linen cloth, morning light"
              className="os-input mt-4 resize-none leading-relaxed"
            />
            <button data-testid="asset-generate" onClick={generate} disabled={busy} className="foil-bg w-full py-3 text-sm font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mt-4 disabled:opacity-50">
              <ImagePlus className="h-4 w-4" />{busy ? "Generating…" : "Generate image"}
            </button>
            <div className="mt-5">
              <div className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F] mb-2">Try one</div>
              <div className="space-y-1">
                {IDEAS.map((i) => (
                  <button key={i} onClick={() => setPrompt(i)} className="block text-left text-[11px] text-[#8D897F] hover:text-[#D4AF37] transition-colors duration-200">
                    {i}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="lg:col-span-8">
          {assets.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="border border-dashed border-white/15 p-10" data-testid="assets-empty">
              <div className="font-display text-3xl">No generated imagery yet</div>
              <p className="text-sm text-[#8D897F] mt-3 max-w-md">
                Generated images land here permanently and appear in the console's Images tab, ready to drop into any slot
                of any template — the manifest marks them as AI assets.
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
              {assets.map((a) => (
                <figure key={a.id} className="border border-white/[0.08] bg-[#0B0B0A]" data-testid={`asset-${a.id}`}>
                  {String(a.url || "").includes("media.base44.com") ? (
                    <Image src={a.url} alt={a.prompt} fittingType="fill" className="w-full aspect-square object-cover" />
                  ) : (
                    <img src={a.url} alt={a.prompt} className="w-full aspect-square object-cover" />
                  )}
                  <figcaption className="p-4">
                    <div className="text-xs text-[#CFCBC1] line-clamp-2">{a.prompt}</div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mt-3 text-[#8D897F]">
                      <a href={a.url} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" className="hover:text-[#D4AF37]"><ExternalLink className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /></a>
                      <button onClick={() => copy(a.url)} className="hover:text-[#D4AF37]"><Copy className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /></button>
                      <button onClick={() => remove(a)} className="ml-auto hover:text-red-400" data-testid={`asset-delete-${a.id}`}><Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /></button>
                    </div>
                  </figcaption>
                </figure>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## 116. src/pages/BriefStudio.jsx

```jsx
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { Sparkles } from "lucide-react";
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";
import { FAMILIES } from "@/lib/catalog";
import { PageHead } from "@/components/os/Shell";
import BriefView from "@/components/os/BriefView";
import { createBrief, getBrief, listBriefs } from "@/lib/store";

export default function BriefStudio() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const [params, setParams] = useSearchParams();
  const [prompt, setPrompt] = useState("");
  const [family, setFamily] = useState("consumer");
  const [brief, setBrief] = useState(null);
  const [briefId, setBriefId] = useState(params.get("brief") || "");
  const [images, setImages] = useState({});
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const { data: briefs = [] } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["briefs"], queryFn: listBriefs });

  useEffect(() => {
    const id = params.get("brief");
    if (!id) return;
    let alive = true;
    getBrief(id)
      .then((b) => {
        if (!alive || !b) return;
        setBrief(b);
        setBriefId(b.id);
        setImages(b.images || {});
      })
      .catch(() => {});
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, [params]);

  const write = async () => {
    if (prompt.trim().length < 8) return toast.error("Tell the studio about the business first.");
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const res = await base44.functions.invoke("generateBrief", { prompt: prompt.trim(), family });
      const data = res.data || {};
      if (data.error) throw new Error(data.error);
      const saved = await createBrief({ ...data.brief, family, source_prompt: prompt.trim(), images: {} });
      setBrief(saved);
      setBriefId(saved.id);
      setImages({});
      setParams({ brief: saved.id });
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["briefs"] });
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["ledger"] });
      toast.success(`“${saved.title}” written — apply it to any matching template`);
    } catch (e) {
      toast.error(e.message || "The studio could not answer. Try again.");
    }
    setBusy(false);
  };

  return (
    <div>
      <PageHead no="08" eyebrow="AI brief studio" title="From a sentence to a direction">
        <div className="max-w-md">
          <div className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F] mb-2">Family the brief is written for</div>
          <select value={family} onChange={(e) => setFamily(e.target.value)} data-testid="brief-family" className="os-select">
            {FAMILIES.map(([v, l]) => (
              <option key={v} value={v}>{l}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
      </PageHead>

      <div className="px-6 md:px-14 py-10">
        <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-12 gap-10">
          <div className="lg:col-span-5">
            <div className="bg-[#0B0B0A] border border-white/[0.08] p-6">
              <div className="eyebrow text-[#D4AF37]">The business, in your own words</div>
              <textarea
                data-testid="brief-prompt"
                value={prompt}
                onChange={(e) => setPrompt(e.target.value)}
                rows={7}
                placeholder="A seaside oyster bar in Galway. Twelve tables, one shucker, the catch comes in Tuesday to Saturday. We want people to book a table rather than phone us."
                className="os-input mt-4 resize-none leading-relaxed"
              />
              <button data-testid="brief-generate" onClick={write} disabled={busy} className="foil-bg w-full py-3 text-sm font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mt-4 disabled:opacity-50">
                <Sparkles className="h-4 w-4" />{busy ? "Writing the brief…" : "Write the brief"}
              </button>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-[#8D897F] mt-4 leading-relaxed">
                Returns positioning, audience, headline, subhead and call to action, two or three buyer paths, a real
                palette, the module list, the page order and a QA checklist — then saves it to your account.
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-8">
              <div className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F] mb-3">Briefs in the OS</div>
              {briefs.length === 0 && <p className="text-xs text-[#8D897F]">No briefs yet.</p>}
              <div className="space-y-2">
                {briefs.map((b) => (
                  <button
                    key={b.id}
                    data-testid={`brief-item-${b.id}`}
                    onClick={() => {
                      setBrief(b);
                      setBriefId(b.id);
                      setImages(b.images || {});
                      setParams({ brief: b.id });
                    }}
                    className={`w-full text-left border p-3 transition-colors duration-200 ${briefId === b.id ? "border-[#D4AF37]" : "border-white/[0.08] hover:border-white/25"}`}
                  >
                    <div className="text-sm">{b.title}</div>
                    <div className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F] mt-1">
                      {b.family} · {b.tier || "—"} {b.images?.hero ? "· images" : ""}
                    </div>
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="lg:col-span-7">
            {brief ? (
              <BriefView brief={brief} family={brief.family || family} briefId={briefId} images={images} onImages={setImages} />
            ) : (
              <div className="border border-dashed border-white/15 p-10" data-testid="brief-empty">
                <div className="font-display text-3xl">No brief open</div>
                <p className="text-sm text-[#8D897F] mt-3 max-w-md">
                  Describe a business on the left, or pick one of your saved briefs. Every brief keeps its own palette,
                  imagery and QA checklist, and carries them into any matching template.
                </p>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## 117. src/pages/ClientShare.jsx

```jsx
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Link, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { BadgeCheck, MessageSquare } from "lucide-react";
import { editionLabel, templateBySlug } from "@/lib/catalog";
import { TokensProvider } from "@/context/TokensContext";
import TemplateFrame from "@/components/os/TemplateFrame";
import ReviewLayer from "@/components/os/ReviewLayer";
import Seal from "@/components/os/Seal";
import { listComments, projectByCode } from "@/lib/store";

export default function ClientShare() {
  const { code } = useParams();
  const [project, setProject] = useState(null);
  const [state, setState] = useState("loading");
  const captureRef = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    projectByCode(code)
      .then((p) => {
        if (!alive) return;
        setProject(p);
        setState(p ? "ready" : "missing");
      })
      .catch(() => alive && setState("missing"));
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, [code]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (state !== "missing") return undefined;
    const meta = document.createElement("meta");
    meta.name = "prerender-status-code";
    meta.content = "404";
    document.head.appendChild(meta);
    return () => {
      document.head.removeChild(meta);
    };
  }, [state]);

  const { data: comments = [] } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["comments", project?.id],
    queryFn: () => listComments(project.id),
    enabled: Boolean(project?.id),
  });

  if (state === "loading") {
    return <div className="min-h-screen grid place-items-center bg-[#070707] text-[#8D897F] text-sm">Opening the share link…</div>;
  }

  if (state === "missing") {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen grid place-items-center bg-[#070707] text-[#F2EFE6] px-6">
        <div className="text-center">
          <div className="font-display text-4xl">That link has no destination</div>
          <p className="text-sm text-[#8D897F] mt-4">
            The share code <span className="font-mono-os text-[#D4AF37]">{code}</span> doesn't match a direction in the OS.
            Ask for a fresh link.
          </p>
          <Link to="/" className="text-sm text-[#D4AF37] inline-block mt-6">Back to OmniForge</Link>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const meta = templateBySlug(project.template_slug);
  const tokens = project.tokens || {};
  const open = comments.filter((c) => !c.resolved).length;

  return (
    <div className="grain min-h-screen bg-[#070707] text-[#F2EFE6] flex flex-col">
      <header className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4 px-6 py-4 border-b border-white/[0.08]">
        <Seal seed={project.share_code} label={project.name} size={44} testid="share-seal" />
        <div>
          <div className="eyebrow text-[#D4AF37]">Shared for review · {project.share_code}</div>
          <div className="font-display text-3xl leading-none mt-2">{project.name}</div>
          <div className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F] mt-2">
            {meta?.name || project.template_slug} · {editionLabel(project.template_slug)} · {open} open note{open === 1 ? "" : "s"}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-3 text-[11px]">
          {project.approval_name ? (
            <span className="flex items-center gap-2 border border-[#C6FF3D]/50 text-[#C6FF3D] px-3 py-2" data-testid="share-approval">
              <BadgeCheck className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />Signed by {project.approval_name}
              {project.approved_at ? ` · ${new Date(project.approved_at).toLocaleDateString()}` : ""}
            </span>
          ) : (
            <span className="flex items-center gap-2 border border-white/15 px-3 py-2 text-[#8D897F]">
              <MessageSquare className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />Awaiting signature
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
      </header>

      <div className="relative flex-1 min-h-0">
        <TokensProvider slug={project.template_slug} tokens={tokens}>
          <TemplateFrame slug={project.template_slug} tokens={tokens} captureRef={captureRef} testid="share-frame" />
          <ReviewLayer project={project} />
        </TokensProvider>
      </div>

      <footer className="px-6 py-5 border-t border-white/[0.08] flex flex-wrap items-center gap-6">
        <div className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F]">
          Pin notes on the page, then sign the approval. {project.approval_note ? `Note: “${project.approval_note}”` : ""}
        </div>
        <div className="ml-auto eyebrow text-[#D4AF37]">OmniForge · Edition {project.share_code}</div>
      </footer>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## 118. src/pages/Compare.jsx

```jsx
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { ArrowLeftRight } from "lucide-react";
import { CATALOG, templateBySlug, editionLabel } from "@/lib/catalog";
import { COLOR_KEYS, TEMPLATE_TOKENS, fontPair, prestigeScore } from "@/lib/tokens";
import { PageHead } from "@/components/os/Shell";
import TemplateFrame from "@/components/os/TemplateFrame";
import { listProjects } from "@/lib/store";

const OPTIONS = [
  ...CATALOG.map((t) => ({ value: `t:${t.slug}`, label: `${t.name} — catalogue default` })),
];

const resolve = (value, projects) => {
  if (!value) return null;
  if (value.startsWith("t:")) {
    const slug = value.slice(2);
    const meta = templateBySlug(slug);
    return { slug, name: meta?.name || slug, tokens: (TEMPLATE_TOKENS[slug] || TEMPLATE_TOKENS["saas-orbit"]).defaults, kind: "Template" };
  }
  const p = projects.find((x) => x.id === value);
  if (!p) return null;
  return { slug: p.template_slug, name: p.name, tokens: { ...(TEMPLATE_TOKENS[p.template_slug] || TEMPLATE_TOKENS["saas-orbit"]).defaults, ...(p.tokens || {}) }, kind: "Project", id: p.id, status: p.status };
};

const Row = ({ label, a, b, type }) => {
  const same = String(a) === String(b);
  return (
    <tr className={`border-b border-white/[0.06] ${same ? "" : "bg-[#D4AF37]/[0.04]"}`}>
      <td className="py-2 pr-4 eyebrow text-[#8D897F]">{label}</td>
      <td className="py-2 pr-6">
        {type === "color" ? (
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="h-4 w-4 border border-white/20" style={{ background: a }} />
            <span className="font-mono-os text-[11px]">{String(a).toUpperCase()}</span>
          </span>
        ) : (
          <span className="text-sm">{String(a)}</span>
        )}
      </td>
      <td className="py-2">
        {type === "color" ? (
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="h-4 w-4 border border-white/20" style={{ background: b }} />
            <span className="font-mono-os text-[11px]">{String(b).toUpperCase()}</span>
          </span>
        ) : (
          <span className="text-sm">{String(b)}</span>
        )}
      </td>
    </tr>
  );
};

export default function Compare() {
  const [params] = useSearchParams();
  const { data: projects = [] } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["projects"], queryFn: listProjects });
  const [a, setA] = useState(params.get("a") || "");
  const [b, setB] = useState(params.get("b") || "");

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!params.get("a")) setA("t:saas-orbit");
    if (!params.get("b")) setB("t:cg-maison");
  }, [params]);

  const left = useMemo(() => resolve(a, projects), [a, projects]);
  const right = useMemo(() => resolve(b, projects), [b, projects]);

  const options = [...OPTIONS, ...projects.map((p) => ({ value: p.id, label: `${p.name} — project` }))];
  const side = (value, set, resolved, testid) => (
    <div className="flex-1 min-w-[220px]">
      <select value={value} onChange={(e) => set(e.target.value)} data-testid={testid} className="os-select">
        {options.map((o) => (
          <option key={o.value} value={o.value}>{o.label}</option>
        ))}
      </select>
      {resolved && (
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mt-3">
          <span className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F]">{resolved.kind} · {editionLabel(resolved.slug)}</span>
          <span className="eyebrow text-[#D4AF37]">Prestige {prestigeScore(resolved.tokens).score}</span>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );

  return (
    <div>
      <PageHead no="07" eyebrow="Compare" title="Side by side">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          {side(a, setA, left, "compare-a")}
          <ArrowLeftRight className="h-4 w-4 text-[#8D897F] mt-1" />
          {side(b, setB, right, "compare-b")}
        </div>
      </PageHead>

      <div className="px-6 md:px-14 py-8">
        <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          {[left, right].map((s, i) => (
            <div key={i} className="h-[420px] border border-white/[0.08] overflow-hidden">
              {s ? <TemplateFrame slug={s.slug} tokens={s.tokens} testid={`compare-frame-${i}`} /> : <div className="p-6 text-sm text-[#8D897F]">Pick something to compare.</div>}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        {left && right && (
          <div className="mt-8 grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-10">
            <table className="w-full" data-testid="compare-table">
              <thead>
                <tr className="border-b border-white/20">
                  <th className="text-left py-2 eyebrow text-[#8D897F]">Token</th>
                  <th className="text-left py-2 eyebrow text-[#D4AF37]">{left.name}</th>
                  <th className="text-left py-2 eyebrow text-[#D4AF37]">{right.name}</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {COLOR_KEYS.map(([k, label]) => (
                  <Row key={k} label={label} a={left.tokens[k]} b={right.tokens[k]} type="color" />
                ))}
                <Row label="Font" a={`${fontPair(left.tokens.font).name}`} b={`${fontPair(right.tokens.font).name}`} />
                <Row label="Radius" a={`${left.tokens.radius}px`} b={`${right.tokens.radius}px`} />
                <Row label="Density" a={Number(left.tokens.space).toFixed(2)} b={Number(right.tokens.space).toFixed(2)} />
                <Row label="Motion" a={left.tokens.motion} b={right.tokens.motion} />
              </tbody>
            </table>
            <div className="text-sm text-[#8D897F] leading-relaxed">
              <div className="eyebrow text-[#D4AF37] mb-3">Reading the diff</div>
              <p>Highlighted rows differ between the two directions — those are the decisions that separate them.</p>
              <p className="mt-4">
                Left is {left.kind === "Project" ? "a saved project" : "the catalogue default"}; right is{" "}
                {right.kind === "Project" ? "a saved project" : "the catalogue default"}.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## 119. src/pages/Configurator.jsx

```jsx
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { ArrowRight, Check } from "lucide-react";
import { CONFIGURATOR, MOTION_VALUES, SPACE_VALUES, templateBySlug } from "@/lib/catalog";
import { TEMPLATE_TOKENS, prestigeScore } from "@/lib/tokens";
import { PageHead } from "@/components/os/Shell";
import TemplateFrame from "@/components/os/TemplateFrame";
import Seal from "@/components/os/Seal";
import { createProject } from "@/lib/store";

export default function Configurator() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [answers, setAnswers] = useState({ kind: "saas-orbit", feel: null, space: "standard", motion: "measured", radius: 16 });
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const meta = templateBySlug(answers.kind);
  const tokens = useMemo(() => {
    const d = (TEMPLATE_TOKENS[answers.kind] || TEMPLATE_TOKENS["saas-orbit"]).defaults;
    return {
      ...d,
      font: answers.feel || d.font,
      space: SPACE_VALUES[answers.space],
      motion: MOTION_VALUES[answers.motion],
      radius: answers.radius,
    };
  }, [answers]);
  const prestige = prestigeScore(tokens);
  const answered = ["kind", "feel", "space", "motion", "radius"].filter((k) => answers[k] !== null && answers[k] !== undefined).length;

  const create = async () => {
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const created = await createProject({
        name: name.trim() || `${meta.name} project`,
        family: meta.family,
        tier: meta.tier,
        template_slug: answers.kind,
        modules: meta.modules,
        tokens,
        prestige: prestige.score,
      });
      toast.success(`“${created.name}” created with its first seven decisions made.`);
      navigate(`/templates/${answers.kind}?project=${created.id}&tune=1`);
    } catch (e) {
      toast.error(e.message || "The project could not be created.");
    }
    setBusy(false);
  };

  const chip = (on) => `text-left px-4 py-3 border transition-colors duration-200 ${on ? "border-[#D4AF37] bg-[#D4AF37]/[0.07]" : "border-white/10 hover:border-white/30"}`;

  return (
    <div>
      <PageHead no="05" eyebrow="Configurator" title="Five answers, a project">
        <div className="text-right">
          <div className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F]">Matched template</div>
          <div className="font-display text-3xl mt-1">{meta.name}</div>
          <div className="eyebrow text-[#D4AF37] mt-1">Prestige {prestige.score} · {prestige.grade}</div>
        </div>
      </PageHead>

      <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-12 gap-10 px-6 md:px-14 py-10">
        <div className="lg:col-span-5 space-y-10">
          {CONFIGURATOR.map((q) => (
            <section key={q.id} data-testid={`configurer-${q.id}`}>
              <div className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F]">{q.no}</div>
              <h2 className="font-display text-3xl mt-2 mb-5">{q.prompt}</h2>
              <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                {q.options.map((o) => {
                  const value = o.value;
                  const on = answers[q.id] === value;
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={String(value)}
                      data-testid={`conf-${q.id}-${value}`}
                      className={chip(on)}
                      onClick={() => setAnswers((a) => ({ ...a, [q.id]: value, ...(q.id === "kind" ? { feel: null } : {}) }))}
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span className={`h-2 w-2 ${on ? "bg-[#D4AF37]" : "bg-white/20"}`} />
                        <span className="text-sm">{o.label}</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-[11px] text-[#8D897F] mt-1 ml-4">{o.hint}</div>
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </section>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="lg:col-span-7">
          <div className="relative rounded-none border border-white/[0.08] overflow-hidden h-[560px]">
            <TemplateFrame slug={answers.kind} tokens={tokens} testid="configurator-frame" />
          </div>
          <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap items-end gap-4">
            <div className="flex-1 min-w-[220px]">
              <div className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F] mb-2">Name the project</div>
              <input
                data-testid="configurator-name"
                value={name}
                onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
                placeholder={`${meta.name} project`}
                className="os-input"
              />
            </div>
            <button data-testid="configurator-create" onClick={create} disabled={busy} className="foil-bg px-6 py-3 text-sm font-semibold flex items-center gap-2 disabled:opacity-50">
              {busy ? "Creating…" : "Create project"} <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />
            </button>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <Seal seed={answers.kind} label={meta.name} size={40} testid="configurator-seal" />
              <span className="text-[11px] text-[#8D897F]">{answered} / 5 answered</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <ul className="mt-5 grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-x-8">
            {prestige.checks.map((c) => (
              <li key={c.label} className="flex justify-between text-[11px] py-1 border-b border-white/[0.06]">
                <span className={c.pass ? "text-[#CFCBC1]" : "text-[#FF8A7A]"}>{c.pass ? <Check className="inline h-3 w-3 mr-1" /> : "! "}{c.label}</span>
                <span className="font-mono-os text-[#8D897F]">{c.ratio.toFixed(1)}:1 / {c.target}</span>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## 120. src/pages/ForgotPassword.jsx

```jsx
import React, { useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Mail, ArrowLeft, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import AuthLayout from "@/components/AuthLayout";

export default function ForgotPassword() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [sent, setSent] = useState(false);

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await base44.auth.resetPasswordRequest(email);
    } catch {
      // Always show success regardless
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
      setSent(true);
    }
  };

  return (
    <AuthLayout
      icon={Mail}
      title="Reset password"
      subtitle="We'll send you a link to reset it"
      footer={
        <Link to="/login" className="text-primary font-medium hover:underline">
          <ArrowLeft className="w-3 h-3 inline mr-1" />Back to log in
        </Link>
      }
    >
      {sent ? (
        <p className="text-sm text-foreground text-center">
          If an account exists with that email, you'll receive a password reset link shortly.
        </p>
      ) : (
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="email">Email address</Label>
            <div className="relative">
              <Mail className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden="true" />
              <Input
                id="email"
                type="email"
                autoComplete="email"
                autoFocus
                placeholder="you@example.com"
                value={email}
                onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                className="pl-10 h-12"
                required
              />
            </div>
          </div>
          <Button type="submit" className="w-full h-12 font-medium" disabled={loading}>
            {loading ? (
              <>
                <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin" />
                Sending...
              </>
            ) : (
              "Send reset link"
            )}
          </Button>
        </form>
      )}
    </AuthLayout>
  );
}
```

## 121. src/pages/Login.jsx

```jsx
import React, { useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { LogIn, Mail, Lock, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import AuthLayout from "@/components/AuthLayout";
import GoogleIcon from "@/components/GoogleIcon";
import { safeReturnTo } from "@/lib/authReturnTo";

export default function Login() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  // Post-login destination (e.g. the MCP OAuth consent page sends users here
  // with returnTo so the grant flow can resume). Same-origin paths only.
  const returnTo = safeReturnTo();

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await base44.auth.loginViaEmailPassword(email, password);
      window.location.href = returnTo;
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || "Invalid email or password");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleGoogle = () => {
    base44.auth.loginWithProvider("google", returnTo);
  };

  return (
    <AuthLayout
      icon={LogIn}
      title="Welcome back"
      subtitle="Log in to your account"
      footer={
        <>
          Don't have an account?{" "}
          <Link
            to={"/register" + (returnTo !== "/" ? "?returnTo=" + encodeURIComponent(returnTo) : "")}
            className="text-primary font-medium hover:underline"
          >
            Create one
          </Link>
        </>
      }
    >
      <Button
        variant="outline"
        className="w-full h-12 text-sm font-medium mb-6"
        onClick={handleGoogle}
      >
        <GoogleIcon className="w-5 h-5 mr-2" />
        Continue with Google
      </Button>

      <div className="relative mb-6">
        <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center">
          <div className="w-full border-t border-border" />
        </div>
        <div className="relative flex justify-center text-xs uppercase">
          <span className="bg-card px-3 text-muted-foreground">or</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {error && (
        <div className="mb-4 p-3 rounded-lg bg-destructive/10 text-destructive text-sm">
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <Label htmlFor="email">Email</Label>
          <div className="relative">
            <Mail className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden="true" />
            <Input
              id="email"
              type="email"
              autoComplete="email"
              autoFocus
              placeholder="you@example.com"
              value={email}
              onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
              className="pl-10 h-12"
              required
            />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <Label htmlFor="password">Password</Label>
            <Link to="/forgot-password" className="text-xs text-primary hover:underline">
              Forgot password?
            </Link>
          </div>
          <div className="relative">
            <Lock className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden="true" />
            <Input
              id="password"
              type="password"
              autoComplete="current-password"
              placeholder="••••••••"
              value={password}
              onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
              className="pl-10 h-12"
              required
            />
          </div>
        </div>
        <Button type="submit" className="w-full h-12 font-medium" disabled={loading}>
          {loading ? (
            <>
              <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin" />
              Logging in...
            </>
          ) : (
            "Log in"
          )}
        </Button>
      </form>
    </AuthLayout>
  );
}
```

```jsx
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { MODULES, templateBySlug } from "@/lib/catalog";
import { PageHead } from "@/components/os/Shell";

export default function Modules() {
  const groups = useMemo(() => [...new Set(MODULES.map((m) => m.group))], []);
  const [group, setGroup] = useState("all");
  const list = MODULES.filter((m) => (group === "all" ? true : m.group === group));
  const chip = (on) => `px-4 py-2 text-xs border transition-colors duration-200 ${on ? "border-[#D4AF37] text-[#D4AF37] bg-[#D4AF37]/[0.07]" : "border-white/10 text-[#8D897F] hover:text-[#F2EFE6] hover:border-white/30"}`;

  return (
    <div>
      <PageHead no="04" eyebrow="Module gallery" title="Sections, in isolation">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <button data-testid="module-filter-all" className={chip(group === "all")} onClick={() => setGroup("all")}>All</button>
          {groups.map((g) => (
            <button key={g} data-testid={`module-filter-${g}`} className={chip(group === g)} onClick={() => setGroup(g)}>{g}</button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </PageHead>

      <div className="px-6 md:px-14 py-10">
        <div className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F] mb-6">{list.length} modules, each one live inside a flagship</div>

            const t = templateBySlug(m.slug);
            return (
              <div key={m.id} className="bg-[#0B0B0A] border border-white/[0.08] p-6 tilt flex flex-col" data-testid={`module-${m.id}`}>
                <div className="eyebrow text-[#D4AF37]">{m.group}</div>
                <div className="font-display text-2xl mt-3">{m.name}</div>
                <p className="text-sm text-[#8D897F] mt-3 flex-1">{m.note}</p>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 mt-6 pt-4 border-t border-white/[0.08]">
                  <span className="text-xs text-[#8D897F]">in {t?.name}</span>
                  <Link to={`/templates/${m.slug}`} className="text-xs border border-white/15 px-3 py-1.5 hover:border-[#D4AF37] transition-colors duration-200">
                    See it live
                  </Link>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```
