## 123. src/pages/OAuthConsent.jsx

```jsx
import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { appParams } from "@/lib/app-params";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { ShieldCheck, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import AuthLayout from "@/components/AuthLayout";

const OPERATION_VERBS = { read: "view", create: "create", update: "edit", delete: "delete" };
const COLLAPSED_DATA_ROWS = 6;

// Entity tools arrive one per operation; show each entity once, naming only the
// operations it actually grants.
function groupTools(tools) {
  const operationsByEntity = new Map();
  const agents = [];
  const actions = [];
  for (const tool of tools) {
    if (tool.kind === "entity" && tool.entity) {
      if (!operationsByEntity.has(tool.entity)) operationsByEntity.set(tool.entity, []);
      operationsByEntity.get(tool.entity).push(tool.operation);
    } else if (tool.kind === "agent") {
      agents.push(tool);
    } else {
      actions.push(tool);
    }
  }
  const data = [...operationsByEntity].map(([entity, operations]) => ({
    entity,
    operations: Object.keys(OPERATION_VERBS).filter((op) => operations.includes(op)),
  }));
  return { data, agents, actions };
}

const humanize = (name) =>
  name.split(/[_-]+/).filter(Boolean).map((word) => word[0].toUpperCase() + word.slice(1)).join(" ") || name;

// "View, create and delete records" — delete is emphasized so it doesn't hide
// among harmless verbs.
function OperationSummary({ operations }) {
  return (
    <>
      {operations.map((op, i) => {
        const verb = OPERATION_VERBS[op];
        const separator = i === 0 ? "" : i === operations.length - 1 ? " and " : ", ";
        return (
          <React.Fragment key={op}>
            {separator}
            <span className={op === "delete" ? "font-medium text-destructive" : undefined}>
              {i === 0 ? verb[0].toUpperCase() + verb.slice(1) : verb}
            </span>
          </React.Fragment>
        );
      })}{" "}
      records
    </>
  );
}

function ToolSection({ title, children }) {
  return (
    <section>
      <h3 className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground mb-2">{title}</h3>
      <ul className="divide-y divide-border rounded-lg border border-border">{children}</ul>
    </section>
  );
}

function ToolRow({ title, children }) {
  return (
    <li className="flex flex-col gap-0.5 px-3 py-2">
      <span className="font-medium text-foreground">{title}</span>
      {children && <span className="text-muted-foreground">{children}</span>}
    </li>
  );
}

// App-side OAuth consent page for the app's MCP server. The platform redirects
// AI clients here (see base44/mcp/config.json `consent_path`) with an opaque
// `ctx` handle — the authorization request itself lives on the server. This page
// gates on the app-user session, fetches the display info for that handle, shows
// the categories of access being granted, and posts the approve/deny decision.
// Do not change the fetch calls, headers, or the `ctx` handle handling — styling
// and copy are safe to edit.
export default function OAuthConsent() {
  const ctx = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("ctx");
  const [info, setInfo] = useState(null);
  const [checking, setChecking] = useState(true);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [decided, setDecided] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [reconnect, setReconnect] = useState("");
  const [showAllData, setShowAllData] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      let redirecting = false;
      try {
        if (!ctx) {
          setError("This authorization link is invalid or has expired.");
          return;
        }
        // Resolve the handle first: a dead handle must never render
        // approve/deny, and the response carries the app's configured login
        // route for the signed-out redirect below. Send the session (cookie +
        // bearer token) so the server can list the granted tools for a
        // signed-in user — the same auth the approve/deny call sends; without
        // it the display request is anonymous and shows no tools.
        const infoHeaders = {};
        if (appParams.token) infoHeaders.Authorization = "Bearer " + appParams.token;
        const res = await fetch(
          `/api/apps/${appParams.appId}/mcp/consent-info?handle=${encodeURIComponent(ctx)}`,
          { credentials: "include", headers: infoHeaders },
        );
        if (!res.ok) {
          setError("This authorization link is invalid or has expired.");
          return;
        }
        const data = await res.json();
        // Gate on the server's auth result, NOT base44.auth.isAuthenticated():
        // the SDK check runs the bearer path, so a cookie-only session (platform
        // login/SSO, or a private app with a stale localStorage token) would read
        // as signed-out and redirect — even though /consent-info just
        // authenticated this same request via its cookie fallback. data.authenticated
        // keeps the redirect decision in agreement with what the server returned.
        if (!data.authenticated) {
          // The short handle rides back in returnTo; login_path is
          // owner-configured and validated server-side as a same-origin path.
          // Send from_url too: a custom-auth app coerced to platform auth (e.g.
          // public_without_login under workspace SSO) serves the platform login,
          // which honors from_url rather than returnTo. Rebuild the query from
          // `ctx` alone — never forward window.location.search raw: the platform
          // resume returns from_url verbatim, so crafted extras on the consent
          // link (app_base_url, access_token, …) would ride through the login
          // round-trip and app-params.js would persist them into the freshly
          // authenticated session.
          const returnTo =
            window.location.pathname + "?ctx=" + encodeURIComponent(ctx);
          const encoded = encodeURIComponent(returnTo);
          redirecting = true; // keep the spinner while the browser navigates
          window.location.href =
            (data.login_path || "/login") + "?returnTo=" + encoded + "&from_url=" + encoded;
          return;
        }
        setInfo(data);
      } catch (e) {
        setError("Could not load this authorization request. Please try again.");
      } finally {
        if (!redirecting) setChecking(false);
      }
    })();
  }, [ctx]);

  const respond = async (action) => {
    setSubmitting(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      const headers = { "Content-Type": "application/json" };
      // Cookie-backed sessions carry no token; sending "Bearer null" would
      // shadow the valid cookie, so add the header only when a token exists.
      if (appParams.token) headers.Authorization = "Bearer " + appParams.token;
      const res = await fetch(`/api/apps/${appParams.appId}/mcp/authorize-grant`, {
        method: "POST",
        credentials: "include",
        headers,
        body: JSON.stringify({ ctx, action }),
      });
      if (!res.ok) {
        // 401 = the session expired before the (single-use, still-unconsumed)
        // handle was spent; retrying the same controls re-sends the dead session
        // forever. Send the user back through login preserving `ctx` — the same
        // redirect the initial signed-out path uses — so they can return and
        // approve the still-valid handle.
        if (res.status === 401) {
          const returnTo = window.location.pathname + "?ctx=" + encodeURIComponent(ctx);
          const encoded = encodeURIComponent(returnTo);
          window.location.href =
            ((info && info.login_path) || "/login") + "?returnTo=" + encoded + "&from_url=" + encoded;
          return;
        }
        // These all come AFTER the single-use handle is atomically consumed
        // (409 tool set changed; 403 host/resource/app mismatch; 404 access
        // gone; 400 malformed/handle already used), so retrying can only 404.
        // Show a terminal reconnect state, not an impossible "try again".
        if ([400, 403, 404, 409].includes(res.status)) {
          let detail = "";
          try { detail = (await res.json()).detail; } catch (_) { /* keep default */ }
          setReconnect(detail || "This authorization can no longer be completed. Reconnect from your AI client to try again.");
          setSubmitting(false);
          return;
        }
        throw new Error("Could not complete authorization. Please try again.");
      }
      const data = await res.json();
      window.location.href = data.redirect_url;
      if (!/^https?:/i.test(data.redirect_url)) {
        // Custom-scheme redirect (native AI clients, e.g. cursor://): browsers
        // may block or not visibly navigate, so show a terminal state instead
        // of an eternal spinner.
        setDecided(action);
        setSubmitting(false);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e.message);
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  if (checking) {
    return (
      <AuthLayout icon={ShieldCheck} title="Authorize access">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-6 text-muted-foreground">
          <Loader2 className="w-5 h-5 mr-2 animate-spin" aria-hidden="true" />
          Loading…
        </div>
      </AuthLayout>
    );
  }

  const client = (info && info.client_name) || "An AI client";
  const appName = (info && info.app_name) || "this app";

  if (decided) {
    return (
      <AuthLayout
        icon={ShieldCheck}
        title={decided === "approve" ? "Access granted" : "Access denied"}
        subtitle={`You can return to ${client} and close this window.`}
      />
    );
  }

  // Terminal: the authorization request is no longer valid (tool set changed +
  // handle consumed). Retrying can't succeed, so show reconnect guidance with
  // no approve/deny controls.
  if (reconnect) {
    return (
      <AuthLayout icon={ShieldCheck} title="Reconnect required">
        <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-destructive/10 text-destructive text-sm">
          {reconnect}
        </div>
      </AuthLayout>
    );
  }

  // No consent details means nothing trustworthy to approve: a failed
  // consent-info load (expired handle, rate limit, transient error) renders
  // the error alone, never the approve/deny controls.
  if (error && !info) {
    return (
      <AuthLayout icon={ShieldCheck} title="Authorize access">
        <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-destructive/10 text-destructive text-sm">
          {error}
        </div>
      </AuthLayout>
    );
  }

  const tools = Array.isArray(info.tools) ? info.tools : [];
  const { data, agents, actions } = groupTools(tools);

  return (
    <AuthLayout
      icon={ShieldCheck}
      title="Authorize access"
      subtitle={`${client} wants to access ${appName} on your behalf`}
    >
      {error && (
        <div className="mb-4 p-3 rounded-lg bg-destructive/10 text-destructive text-sm">
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      <p className="text-sm font-medium text-foreground mb-3">
        {tools.length ? `${client} will be able to:` : "No tools requested"}
      </p>
      {tools.length > 0 && (
        <div className="space-y-4 text-sm mb-6">
          {data.length > 0 && (
            <ToolSection title={`Data (${data.length})`}>
              {(showAllData ? data : data.slice(0, COLLAPSED_DATA_ROWS)).map(({ entity, operations }) => (
                <ToolRow key={entity} title={entity}>
                  <OperationSummary operations={operations} />
                </ToolRow>
              ))}
              {data.length > COLLAPSED_DATA_ROWS && (
                <li className="px-3 py-2">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="text-sm font-medium text-primary hover:underline"
                    onClick={() => setShowAllData((shown) => !shown)}
                  >
                    {showAllData ? "Show less" : `Show all ${data.length}`}
                  </button>
                </li>
              )}
            </ToolSection>
          )}
          {agents.length > 0 && (
            <ToolSection title="Agents">
              {agents.map((tool) => (
                <ToolRow key={tool.name} title={tool.agent ? humanize(tool.agent) : tool.title || tool.name}>
                  {tool.description}
                </ToolRow>
              ))}
            </ToolSection>
          )}
          {actions.length > 0 && (
            <ToolSection title="Actions">
              {actions.map((tool) => (
                <ToolRow key={tool.name} title={tool.title || tool.name}>
                  {tool.description}
                </ToolRow>
              ))}
            </ToolSection>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="flex gap-3">
        <Button
          variant="outline"
          className="flex-1 h-12 font-medium"
          disabled={submitting}
          onClick={() => respond("deny")}
        >
          Deny
        </Button>
        <Button
          className="flex-1 h-12 font-medium"
          disabled={submitting}
          onClick={() => respond("approve")}
        >
          {submitting ? <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin" /> : null}
          Approve
        </Button>
      </div>
    </AuthLayout>
  );
}
```

## 124. src/pages/Projects.jsx

```jsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { Link, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { Trash2, Copy, Columns2, SlidersHorizontal, BadgeCheck, Clock } from "lucide-react";
import { editionLabel, templateBySlug } from "@/lib/catalog";
import { prestigeScore } from "@/lib/tokens";
import { PageHead } from "@/components/os/Shell";
import Seal from "@/components/os/Seal";
import { deleteProject, listProjects } from "@/lib/store";

const STATUS = {
  draft: ["Draft", "text-[#8D897F]", Clock],
  in_review: ["In review", "text-[#F0B429]", Clock],
  approved: ["Approved", "text-[#C6FF3D]", BadgeCheck],
};

export default function Projects() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { data: projects = [], isLoading } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["projects"], queryFn: listProjects });
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState("");

  const shareLink = (code) => `${window.location.origin}/p/${code}`;

  const copy = async (p) => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(shareLink(p.share_code));
      setCopied(p.id);
      toast.success("Client link copied — anyone with the code can open it");
    } catch {
      toast(shareLink(p.share_code));
    }
  };

  const remove = async (p) => {
    await deleteProject(p.id);
    qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["projects"] });
    qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["ledger"] });
    toast.success(`“${p.name}” removed`);
  };

  const tool = "flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs border border-white/15 px-3 py-2 hover:border-[#D4AF37] transition-colors duration-200";

  return (
    <div>
      <PageHead no="06" eyebrow="Projects" title="Saved directions">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
          <Link to="/configurator" className="foil-bg px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold">Configure a new project</Link>
          <Link to="/compare" className="border border-white/20 px-5 py-3 text-sm hover:border-[#D4AF37] transition-colors duration-200">Compare two</Link>
        </div>
      </PageHead>

      <div className="px-6 md:px-14 py-10">
        {isLoading && <p className="text-sm text-[#8D897F]">Reading the archive…</p>}
        {!isLoading && projects.length === 0 && (
          <div className="border border-dashed border-white/15 p-10 max-w-xl" data-testid="projects-empty">
            <div className="font-display text-3xl">Nothing saved yet</div>
            <p className="text-sm text-[#8D897F] mt-3">
              Open any template, tune it in the token console and save the direction. It will appear here with its own share
              code, review notes and approval.
            </p>
            <Link to="/templates" className="text-sm text-[#D4AF37] inline-block mt-4">Open the library →</Link>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
          {projects.map((p) => {
            const meta = templateBySlug(p.template_slug);
            const status = STATUS[p.status || "draft"] || STATUS.draft;
            const [label, cls, Icon] = status;
            const score = p.tokens ? prestigeScore({ ...p.tokens }).score : null;
            return (
              <div key={p.id} className="bg-[#0B0B0A] border border-white/[0.08] p-6 flex flex-col" data-testid={`project-${p.id}`}>
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <div className="font-display text-3xl leading-none truncate">{p.name}</div>
                    <div className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F] mt-2">{meta?.name || p.template_slug} · {editionLabel(p.template_slug)}</div>
                  </div>
                  <Seal seed={p.share_code || p.id} label={p.name} size={46} testid={`project-seal-${p.id}`} />
                </div>

                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 mt-5 text-[11px]">
                  <span className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 ${cls}`} data-testid={`project-status-${p.id}`}>
                    <Icon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />{label}
                    {p.approval_name ? ` · ${p.approval_name}` : ""}
                  </span>
                  {score !== null && <span className="text-[#8D897F] font-mono-os">prestige {score}</span>}
                  {p.approved_at && <span className="text-[#8D897F] font-mono-os">{new Date(p.approved_at).toLocaleDateString()}</span>}
                </div>

                <div className="mt-5 border border-white/[0.08] px-3 py-2 font-mono-os text-[10px] text-[#8D897F] truncate" data-testid={`project-share-code-${p.id}`}>
                  {shareLink(p.share_code)}
                </div>

                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mt-5 pt-5 border-t border-white/[0.08]">
                  <button className={tool} data-testid={`project-open-${p.id}`} onClick={() => navigate(`/templates/${p.template_slug}?project=${p.id}&tune=1`)}>
                    <SlidersHorizontal className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />Open &amp; export
                  </button>
                  <button className={tool} data-testid={`project-copy-${p.id}`} onClick={() => copy(p)}>
                    <Copy className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />{copied === p.id ? "Copied" : "Client link"}
                  </button>
                  <Link className={tool} data-testid={`project-compare-${p.id}`} to={`/compare?a=${p.id}`}>
                    <Columns2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />Compare
                  </Link>
                  <Link className={tool} to={`/p/${p.share_code}`} data-testid={`project-review-${p.id}`}>
                    Review page
                  </Link>
                  <button className="ml-auto text-[#8D897F] hover:text-red-400 p-2" data-testid={`project-delete-${p.id}`} onClick={() => remove(p)}>
                    <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## 125. src/pages/Register.jsx

```jsx
import React, { useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { UserPlus, Mail, Lock, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import { InputOTP, InputOTPGroup, InputOTPSlot } from "@/components/ui/input-otp";
import AuthLayout from "@/components/AuthLayout";
import GoogleIcon from "@/components/GoogleIcon";
import { toast } from "@/components/ui/use-toast";
import { safeReturnTo } from "@/lib/authReturnTo";

export default function Register() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [showOtp, setShowOtp] = useState(false);
  const [otpCode, setOtpCode] = useState("");

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    if (password !== confirmPassword) {
      setError("Passwords do not match");
      return;
    }
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await base44.auth.register({ email, password });
      setShowOtp(true);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || "Registration failed");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleVerify = async () => {
    setError("");
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const result = await base44.auth.verifyOtp({ email, otpCode });
      if (result?.access_token) {
        base44.auth.setToken(result.access_token);
      }
      window.location.href = safeReturnTo();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || "Invalid verification code");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleResend = async () => {
    setError("");
    try {
      await base44.auth.resendOtp(email);
      toast({
        title: "Code sent",
        description: "Check your email for the new code.",
      });
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || "Failed to resend code");
    }
  };

  const handleGoogle = () => {
    base44.auth.loginWithProvider("google", safeReturnTo());
  };

  if (showOtp) {
    return (
      <AuthLayout
        icon={Mail}
        title="Verify your email"
        subtitle={`We sent a code to ${email}`}
      >
        {error && (
          <div className="mb-4 p-3 rounded-lg bg-destructive/10 text-destructive text-sm">
            {error}
          </div>
        )}
        <div className="flex justify-center mb-6">
          <InputOTP
            maxLength={6}
            value={otpCode}
            onChange={setOtpCode}
            autoFocus
            autoComplete="one-time-code"
          >
            <InputOTPGroup>
              <InputOTPSlot index={0} />
              <InputOTPSlot index={1} />
              <InputOTPSlot index={2} />
              <InputOTPSlot index={3} />
              <InputOTPSlot index={4} />
              <InputOTPSlot index={5} />
            </InputOTPGroup>
          </InputOTP>
        </div>
        <Button
          className="w-full h-12 font-medium"
          onClick={handleVerify}
          disabled={loading || otpCode.length < 6}
        >
          {loading ? (
            <>
              <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin" />
              Verifying...
            </>
          ) : (
            "Verify"
          )}
        </Button>
        <p className="text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground mt-4">
          Didn't receive the code?{" "}
          <button onClick={handleResend} className="text-primary font-medium hover:underline">
            Resend
          </button>
        </p>
      </AuthLayout>
    );
  }

  return (
    <AuthLayout
      icon={UserPlus}
      title="Create your account"
      subtitle="Sign up to get started"
      footer={
        <>
          Already have an account?{" "}
          <Link
            to={"/login" + (safeReturnTo() !== "/" ? "?returnTo=" + encodeURIComponent(safeReturnTo()) : "")}
            className="text-primary font-medium hover:underline"
          >
            Log in
          </Link>
        </>
      }
    >
      <Button
        variant="outline"
        className="w-full h-12 text-sm font-medium mb-6"
        onClick={handleGoogle}
      >
        <GoogleIcon className="w-5 h-5 mr-2" />
        Continue with Google
      </Button>

      <div className="relative mb-6">
        <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center">
          <div className="w-full border-t border-border" />
        </div>
        <div className="relative flex justify-center text-xs uppercase">
          <span className="bg-card px-3 text-muted-foreground">or</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {error && (
        <div className="mb-4 p-3 rounded-lg bg-destructive/10 text-destructive text-sm">
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <Label htmlFor="email">Email</Label>
          <div className="relative">
            <Mail className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden="true" />
            <Input
              id="email"
              type="email"
              autoComplete="email"
              autoFocus
              placeholder="you@example.com"
              value={email}
              onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
              className="pl-10 h-12"
              required
            />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <Label htmlFor="password">Password</Label>
          <div className="relative">
            <Lock className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden="true" />
            <Input
              id="password"
              type="password"
              autoComplete="new-password"
              placeholder="••••••••"
              value={password}
              onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
              className="pl-10 h-12"
              required
            />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <Label htmlFor="confirm">Confirm Password</Label>
          <div className="relative">
            <Lock className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden="true" />
            <Input
              id="confirm"
              type="password"
              autoComplete="new-password"
              placeholder="••••••••"
              value={confirmPassword}
              onChange={(e) => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)}
              className="pl-10 h-12"
              required
            />
          </div>
        </div>
        <Button type="submit" className="w-full h-12 font-medium" disabled={loading}>
          {loading ? (
            <>
              <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin" />
              Creating account...
            </>
          ) : (
            "Create account"
          )}
        </Button>
      </form>
    </AuthLayout>
  );
}
```

## 126. src/pages/ResetPassword.jsx

```jsx
import React, { useState } from "react";
import { Link, useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Lock, Loader2, AlertTriangle } from "lucide-react";
import AuthLayout from "@/components/AuthLayout";

export default function ResetPassword() {
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const resetToken = searchParams.get("token");

  const [newPassword, setNewPassword] = useState("");
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    if (newPassword !== confirmPassword) {
      setError("Passwords do not match");
      return;
    }
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await base44.auth.resetPassword({ resetToken, newPassword });
      window.location.href = "/login";
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || "Failed to reset password");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  if (!resetToken) {
    return (
      <AuthLayout
        icon={AlertTriangle}
        title="Invalid reset link"
        subtitle="This password reset link is missing or invalid"
        footer={
          <Link to="/forgot-password" className="text-primary font-medium hover:underline">
            Request a new link
          </Link>
        }
      >
        <p className="text-sm text-foreground text-center">
          The link you used appears to be incomplete. Please request a new password reset email.
        </p>
      </AuthLayout>
    );
  }

  return (
    <AuthLayout
      icon={Lock}
      title="New password"
      subtitle="Enter your new password below"
    >
      {error && (
        <div className="mb-4 p-3 rounded-lg bg-destructive/10 text-destructive text-sm">
          {error}
        </div>
      )}
      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <Label htmlFor="password">New Password</Label>
          <div className="relative">
            <Lock className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden="true" />
            <Input
              id="password"
              type="password"
              autoComplete="new-password"
              autoFocus
              placeholder="••••••••"
              value={newPassword}
              onChange={(e) => setNewPassword(e.target.value)}
              className="pl-10 h-12"
              required
            />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <Label htmlFor="confirm">Confirm Password</Label>
          <div className="relative">
            <Lock className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden="true" />
            <Input
              id="confirm"
              type="password"
              autoComplete="new-password"
              placeholder="••••••••"
              value={confirmPassword}
              onChange={(e) => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)}
              className="pl-10 h-12"
              required
            />
          </div>
        </div>
        <Button type="submit" className="w-full h-12 font-medium" disabled={loading}>
          {loading ? (
            <>
              <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin" />
              Resetting...
            </>
          ) : (
            "Reset password"
          )}
        </Button>
      </form>
    </AuthLayout>
  );
}
```


## 128. src/pages/SourceBundle.jsx

```jsx
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Copy, Download, FileCode2 } from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { PageHead } from "@/components/os/Shell";
import { downloadText } from "@/lib/exportSite";

// The bundle is generated from this app's own source and imported as raw text,
// so it ships with the OS and can be downloaded or copied from here.
export default function SourceBundle() {
  const [text, setText] = useState("");
  const [state, setState] = useState("loading");

  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    import("@/omniforge-source.md?raw")
      .then((m) => {
        if (!alive) return;
        setText(String(m.default || ""));
        setState("ready");
      })
      .catch(() => alive && setState("missing"));
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, []);

  const headings = text.split("\n").filter((l) => /^##\s/.test(l));

  const copy = async () => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
      toast.success("The whole bundle is on your clipboard");
    } catch {
      toast.error("Copy failed — use the download instead.");
    }
  };

  return (
    <div>
      <PageHead no="10" eyebrow="Source bundle" title="Every file, one document">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
          <button
            onClick={() => downloadText(text, "omniforge-source.md")}
            disabled={state !== "ready"}
            className="foil-bg px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold flex items-center gap-2 disabled:opacity-40"
            data-testid="bundle-download"
          >
            <Download className="h-4 w-4" />Download .md
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={copy}
            disabled={state !== "ready"}
            className="border border-white/20 px-5 py-3 text-sm hover:border-[#D4AF37] transition-colors duration-200 flex items-center gap-2 disabled:opacity-40"
            data-testid="bundle-copy"
          >
            <Copy className="h-4 w-4" />Copy all
          </button>
        </div>
      </PageHead>

      <div className="px-6 md:px-14 py-10">
        {state === "loading" && <p className="text-sm text-[#8D897F]">Reading the bundle…</p>}
        {state === "missing" && (
          <div className="border border-dashed border-white/15 p-10 max-w-xl">
            <div className="font-display text-3xl">The bundle isn't built yet</div>
            <p className="text-sm text-[#8D897F] mt-3">Ask me to rebuild it and it will appear here as a download.</p>
          </div>
        )}
        {state === "ready" && (
          <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-12 gap-10">
            <div className="lg:col-span-5">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <FileCode2 className="h-5 w-5 text-[#D4AF37]" />
                <div className="font-mono-os text-sm">omniforge-source.md</div>
              </div>
              <div className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F] mt-3">
                {Math.round(text.length / 1024)} KB · {headings.length} files · markdown, file name then its code
              </div>
              <ol className="mt-8 space-y-1 max-h-[60vh] overflow-y-auto no-scrollbar">
                {headings.map((h) => (
                  <li key={h} className="font-mono-os text-[11px] text-[#8D897F] truncate">{h.replace(/^#+\s/, "")}</li>
                ))}
              </ol>
            </div>
            <div className="lg:col-span-7">
              <pre className="bg-[#0B0B0A] border border-white/[0.08] p-5 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-[#CFCBC1] max-h-[70vh] overflow-auto no-scrollbar whitespace-pre-wrap" data-testid="bundle-preview">
                {text.slice(0, 6000)}
                {text.length > 6000 ? "\n\n… the rest is in the download." : ""}
              </pre>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## 129. src/pages/TemplateEditor.jsx

```jsx
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Link, useParams, useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { SlidersHorizontal, Images, MessageSquare, Monitor, Tablet, Smartphone, ArrowLeft, Download } from "lucide-react";
import { TEMPLATE_SLOTS, TEMPLATE_TOKENS, sanitizeBriefTokens } from "@/lib/tokens";
import { editionLabel, modulesFor, templateBySlug } from "@/lib/catalog";
import { TokensProvider } from "@/context/TokensContext";
import TemplateFrame from "@/components/os/TemplateFrame";
import CustomizerPanel from "@/components/customizer/CustomizerPanel";
import ImageManifest from "@/components/os/ImageManifest";
import ReviewLayer from "@/components/os/ReviewLayer";
import ExportPanel from "@/components/os/ExportPanel";
import Seal from "@/components/os/Seal";
import { getBrief, getProject } from "@/lib/store";

const DEVICES = [
  ["desktop", Monitor],
  ["tablet", Tablet],
  ["phone", Smartphone],
];

export default function TemplateEditor() {
  const { slug } = useParams();
  const [params] = useSearchParams();
  const query = params.toString();
  const meta = templateBySlug(slug);
  const defaults = (TEMPLATE_TOKENS[slug] || TEMPLATE_TOKENS["saas-orbit"]).defaults;

  const [tokens, setTokens] = useState(() => ({ ...defaults }));
  const [panel, setPanel] = useState(params.get("tune") === "1" ? "tune" : null);
  const [device, setDevice] = useState("desktop");
  const [project, setProject] = useState(null);
  const [brief, setBrief] = useState(null);
  const [review, setReview] = useState(params.get("review") === "1");
  const captureRef = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    setTokens({ ...defaults });
    setBrief(null);
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [slug]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const pid = params.get("project");
    const bid = params.get("brief");
    let alive = true;
    (async () => {
      if (pid) {
        const p = await getProject(pid).catch(() => null);
        if (alive && p) {
          setProject(p);
          setTokens({ ...(TEMPLATE_TOKENS[p.template_slug] || { defaults }).defaults, ...(p.tokens || {}) });
        }
      }
      if (bid) {
        const b = await getBrief(bid).catch(() => null);
        if (alive && b) {
          const applied = sanitizeBriefTokens(b.tokens, slug);
          const slots = TEMPLATE_SLOTS[slug] || [];
          const images = { ...(applied.images || {}) };
          if (b.images?.hero && slots[0]) images[slots[0][0]] = b.images.hero;
          if (b.images?.product && slots[1]) images[slots[1][0]] = b.images.product;
          setBrief(b);
          setTokens({ ...applied, images });
          toast.success(`“${b.title}” applied — palette, type and imagery`);
        }
      }
    })();
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [query, slug]);

  const exportProject = useMemo(
    () =>
      project || {
        name: meta ? `${meta.name} direction` : "OmniForge direction",
        template_slug: slug,
        family: meta?.family,
        share_code: "OMNIFORGE1",
      },
    [project, meta, slug]
  );

  if (!meta) {
    return (
      <div className="px-6 md:px-14 py-20">
        <h1 className="font-display text-4xl">That template isn't in the catalogue.</h1>
        <Link to="/templates" className="text-sm text-[#D4AF37] mt-4 inline-block">Back to the library</Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const tool = (on) =>
    `flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 text-xs border transition-colors duration-200 ${
      on ? "border-[#D4AF37] text-[#D4AF37] bg-[#D4AF37]/[0.07]" : "border-white/15 text-[#CFCBC1] hover:border-white/40"
    }`;

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col h-[100dvh]" data-testid="template-editor">
      <header className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4 px-6 py-4 border-b border-white/[0.08]">
        <Link to="/templates" className="text-[#8D897F] hover:text-[#D4AF37] transition-colors duration-200" data-testid="editor-back">
          <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
        </Link>
        <Seal seed={slug} label={meta.name} size={40} testid="editor-seal" />
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <div className="font-display text-2xl leading-none">{meta.name}</div>
          <div className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F] mt-1.5">
            {meta.variant} · {meta.family} · {meta.tier} · {editionLabel(slug)}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 ml-auto">
          <button data-testid="editor-tune" onClick={() => setPanel(panel === "tune" ? null : "tune")} className={tool(panel === "tune")}>
            <SlidersHorizontal className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />Tune
          </button>
          <button data-testid="editor-images" onClick={() => setPanel(panel === "images" ? null : "images")} className={tool(panel === "images")}>
            <Images className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />Images
          </button>
          <button data-testid="editor-export" onClick={() => setPanel(panel === "export" ? null : "export")} className={tool(panel === "export")}>
            <Download className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />Export
          </button>
          <button
            data-testid="editor-review"
            onClick={() => setReview(!review)}
            disabled={!project}
            title={project ? "Open the review layer" : "Save a project first"}
            className={`${tool(review)} disabled:opacity-40`}
          >
            <MessageSquare className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />Review
          </button>
          <div className="flex border border-white/15">
            {DEVICES.map(([d, Icon]) => (
              <button key={d} data-testid={`editor-device-${d}`} onClick={() => setDevice(d)} className={`p-2 ${device === d ? "text-[#D4AF37]" : "text-[#8D897F]"}`}>
                <Icon className="h-4 w-4" />
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 px-6 py-2 border-b border-white/[0.08] text-[11px] text-[#8D897F]">
        {brief && <span className="text-[#D4AF37]" data-testid="editor-brief-chip">Brief applied · {brief.title}</span>}
        {project && <span data-testid="editor-project-chip">Project · {project.name}</span>}
        <span>{modulesFor(slug).length} modules</span>
        <span>{tokens.font}</span>
        <span>{tokens.radius}px radius</span>
        <span>density {Number(tokens.space).toFixed(2)}×</span>
      </div>

      <div className="flex-1 flex min-h-0">
        <TokensProvider slug={slug} tokens={tokens} onTokens={setTokens}>
          <div className="relative flex-1 min-w-0">
            <TemplateFrame slug={slug} tokens={tokens} device={device} captureRef={captureRef} testid="editor-frame" />
            {review && project && <ReviewLayer project={project} />}
          </div>
          {panel === "tune" && <CustomizerPanel onClose={() => setPanel(null)} edition={editionLabel(slug)} />}
          {panel === "images" && <ImageManifest onClose={() => setPanel(null)} />}
          {panel === "export" && (
            <aside className="w-[380px] shrink-0 h-full overflow-y-auto border-l border-white/[0.08] bg-[#0B0B0A] p-5 rise no-scrollbar">
              <ExportPanel
                project={exportProject}
                tokens={tokens}
                captureRef={captureRef}
                onExported={() => toast.success("The edition is on its way down.")}
              />
              <div className="mt-5 text-xs text-[#8D897F] leading-relaxed">
                Tip: pin a couple of review notes, resolve them, then sign the approval on the project's share page — the
                certificate travels with the export.
              </div>
            </aside>
          )}
        </TokensProvider>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## 130. src/pages/TemplateLibrary.jsx

```jsx
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { Search } from "lucide-react";
import { CATALOG, FAMILIES, TIERS, editionLabel, modulesFor } from "@/lib/catalog";
import { TEMPLATE_TOKENS, prestigeScore } from "@/lib/tokens";
import { PageHead } from "@/components/os/Shell";
import Specimen from "@/components/os/Specimen";
import Seal from "@/components/os/Seal";

export default function TemplateLibrary() {
  const [family, setFamily] = useState("all");
  const [tier, setTier] = useState("all");
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");

  const list = useMemo(
    () =>
      CATALOG.filter((t) => (family === "all" ? true : t.family === family))
        .filter((t) => (tier === "all" ? true : t.tier === tier))
        .filter((t) => (q.trim() ? `${t.name} ${t.variant} ${t.blurb}`.toLowerCase().includes(q.trim().toLowerCase()) : true)),
    [family, tier, q]
  );

  const chip = (on) => `px-4 py-2 text-xs border transition-colors duration-200 ${on ? "border-[#D4AF37] text-[#D4AF37] bg-[#D4AF37]/[0.07]" : "border-white/10 text-[#8D897F] hover:text-[#F2EFE6] hover:border-white/30"}`;

  return (
    <div>
      <PageHead no="02" eyebrow="Template library" title="The catalogue">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 border border-white/15 px-4 py-3 min-w-[240px]" data-testid="library-search">
          <Search className="h-4 w-4 text-[#8D897F]" />
          <input value={q} onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)} placeholder="Search the catalogue" className="bg-transparent outline-none text-sm flex-1" />
        </div>
      </PageHead>

      <div className="px-6 md:px-14 py-6 border-b border-white/[0.08] space-y-4">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <span className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F] mr-2">Family</span>
          <button data-testid="filter-family-all" className={chip(family === "all")} onClick={() => setFamily("all")}>All</button>
          {FAMILIES.map(([v, l]) => (
            <button key={v} data-testid={`filter-family-${v}`} className={chip(family === v)} onClick={() => setFamily(v)}>{l}</button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <span className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F] mr-2">Tier</span>
          <button data-testid="filter-tier-all" className={chip(tier === "all")} onClick={() => setTier("all")}>All</button>
          {TIERS.map(([v, l]) => (
            <button key={v} data-testid={`filter-tier-${v}`} className={chip(tier === v)} onClick={() => setTier(v)}>{l}</button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="px-6 md:px-14 py-10">
        <div className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F] mb-6">
          {list.length} template{list.length === 1 ? "" : "s"} · every one drawn from tokens
        </div>
        <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
          {list.map((t) => (
            <div key={t.slug} data-testid={`library-${t.slug}`}>
              <Specimen t={t} testid={`library-specimen-${t.slug}`} />
              <div className="border border-t-0 border-white/[0.08] p-4">
                <p className="text-sm text-[#CFCBC1]">{t.blurb}</p>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mt-4">
                  <Seal seed={t.slug} label={t.name} size={34} color="#D4AF37" testid={`library-seal-${t.slug}`} />
                  <span className="eyebrow text-[#8D897F]">{editionLabel(t.slug)}</span>
                  <span className="ml-auto eyebrow text-[#D4AF37]">Prestige {prestigeScore(TEMPLATE_TOKENS[t.slug].defaults).score}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mt-4">
                  <Link to={`/templates/${t.slug}`} className="text-xs border border-white/15 px-3 py-1.5 hover:border-[#D4AF37] transition-colors duration-200">
                    Open editor
                  </Link>
                  <Link to={`/templates/${t.slug}?tune=1`} className="text-xs border border-white/15 px-3 py-1.5 hover:border-[#D4AF37] transition-colors duration-200">
                    Tune
                  </Link>
                  <span className="text-xs text-[#8D897F] px-1 py-1.5">{modulesFor(t.slug).length} modules</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## 131. src/utils/index.ts

```ts
export function createPageUrl(pageName: string) {
    return '/' + pageName.replace(/ /g, '-');
}
```
